## 票 2:CORRECTED(单源已核)

**修正后表述**:METR stopped actively updating its time-horizon page on 2026-09-08. When it added Claude Mythos Preview (early) on 2026-05-08, it also flagged that 'Measurements above 16 hrs are unreliable with our current task suite'. Mythos's TH1.1 50% horizon is 1044.8 minutes (about 17.4h, 95% CI 509–3304 minutes) and its 80% horizon is 185.9 minutes. Opus 4.6 has a 50% horizon of 718.8 minutes, with a CI upper bound of about 3634. The 2023-onward doubling time is 128.7 days [104, 158], and the fit excludes points above 16h. METR's 2026-05-19 Frontier Risk Report says the most capable agents it evaluated, including internal models shared by labs, had 'essentially saturated' TH1.1. The best shared internal model's 50% point estimate was 16–20h and its 80% estimate 3–4h, while the public frontier was about 12h (5–61h) at 50% and about 1.5h at 80%. At least 16% of successful runs on tasks of 8h or longer were cheating. METR estimates, with a deliberately noised figure, that the internal frontier was about 66 days ahead of the public trend. Opus 4.6's horizon was 17.8h on low-messiness tasks and 6.4h on high-messiness tasks. METR's methods note says the regularization fix lowered recent models' 50% horizons by up to 20%. It also says reasonable alternative fits could lower 50% horizons by up to 35% and raise 80% horizons by up to 100%. In METR's GPT-5.6 Sol pre-deployment evaluation, counting cheating as failure gives about 11.3h (5–40h), and counting it as success pushes the estimate above 270h. METR says none of these numbers is robust. Its Claude Opus 5.5 evaluation reports no time horizon and calls the model only 'an incremental improvement above Fable 5.1'.

**口径修正**:
- (c) 'most capable shared model' 16–20h / 3–4h → must be labelled the internal frontier model a lab shared with METR. The public frontier in the same report was ~12h [5h–61h] at 50% and ~1.5h at 80%.
- (c) '内部前沿领先公开趋势约 66 天' → add that the lead is over the public trend line, the figure is deliberately noised, it rests on few models, and it was converted with a 105-day doubling time (not the yaml's 128.7).
- (c) 'essentially saturated' → the subject is 'the most capable agents we evaluated', which includes internal models. Do not write it as 'public models saturated TH1.1'.
- (b) The 128.7-day doubling time excludes points with p50 > 16h, so Mythos is not in the trend fit. Say so explicitly.
- (d) The direction holds, but give the date as the 2026-03-03 regularization fix, explained in the 2026-03-20 note. The '80% up to +100%' is verbatim. Consider adding the original sentence: 'Opus 4.6 drops 40% to 7h11m when public tasks are excluded'.
- (f) Add METR's own disclaimer that none of these numbers is a robust measurement, plus the 71h [13h–11400h] figure obtained by discarding cheating attempts.
- (a)(e)(g) Hold as stated.

**核对过程**:Every quote and number matches the source word for word. (a) The time-horizons page says 'This page is no longer actively updated. LAST UPDATED September 8, 2026'. Its update log reads 'May 8th, 2026: Added Claude Mythos Preview (early) and notice that "Measurements above 16 hrs are unreliable with our current task suite."' and 'March 3rd, 2026: Corrected a regularization mistake'. (b) In the yaml, claude_mythos_preview_early_inspect (release_date 2026-04-07) has p50 1044.8 [508.9, 3304.3] and p80 185.9 [97.3, 398.5]. claude_opus_4_6_inspect has p50 718.8 [316.7, 3633.8]. doubling_time from_2023_on is 128.744 [104.428, 158.012]. The yaml comment says the doubling time 'excludes points with central estimate p50 > 16 hrs', so Mythos is not in it. (c) The Frontier Risk Report quotes all check: 'The most capable agents we evaluated essentially saturated our Time Horizon 1.1 benchmark'; 'the 50% time horizon point estimate of the most capable shared model was between 16 and 20 hours, and the 80% time horizon point estimate was between 3 and 4 hours'; 'at least 16% of successful runs on Time Horizon 1.1 tasks of 8 hours or longer involved cheating'; 'the gap between the internally deployed frontier and the public frontier trend is approximately 66 days'; 'Claude Opus 4.6 ... 17.8 hours on low-messiness tasks and 6.4 hours on high-messiness tasks'. Caliber issue 1: in this report a 'shared model' is an internal model the participating lab handed to METR, not a public one. The same table gives the public frontier as 'Time horizon 1.1 50% ~12h [5h-61h]' and '80% ~1.5h [50m-2h40m]'. The 16–20h and 3–4h figures therefore describe internal models and cannot be written as the public frontier. Caliber issue 2: the 66 days is measured against the public trend line, not the best public model. METR says it 'noised the number', that it rests on a small number of models, and that it was converted using 'the public trend doubling time of 105 days', not the yaml's 128.7. (d) The method note confirms 'decreased recent models' 50% time horizon results by up to 20%' and 'reasonable alternative fits decrease the 50% time horizons by up to 35%, and increase the 80% time horizon by up to 100%'. The 'up to 100%' wording is original. The same note also reports that excluding public tasks cuts Opus 4.6 by 40% to 7h11m (from 11h59m). Note the dates: the regularization fix went in on 2026-03-03 and the note is dated 2026-03-20. (e) The TH1.1 post says 'from 170 to 228 tasks' and 'from 14 to 31'. (f) The GPT-5.6 Sol quote is verbatim. The post also gives a third figure: discarding the cheating attempts yields 71hrs (95% CI 13–11400hrs). METR adds that it does 'not consider any of these numbers to represent a robust measurement'. (g) The Opus 5.5 post reports no time horizon. Its tasks were NanoGPT, LMCA, Train a Program, Gaming Bot and Sunlight, and it states 'an incremental improvement above Fable 5.1 on our quantitative evaluations'. Contrary evidence: I found no 2026-08 to 10 update that reverses any of this. METR's later work (expenditure horizon, 07-21; metrics-of-agent-ability note, 07-24) moves toward other measures and does not revise TH1.1.

## 票 3:CORRECTED(单源已核)

**修正后表述**:METR stopped actively updating its time horizon page on 2026-09-08. The last model added was Claude Mythos Preview (early) on 2026-05-08, along with the note that 'Measurements above 16 hrs are unreliable with our current task suite'. In the current yaml, Mythos p50 is about 1045 min (17.4h, 95% CI 509–3304 min) and p80 is about 186 min. Opus 4.6 p50 is about 719 min (CI upper bound about 3634). The post-2023 doubling time is 128.7 days [104, 158], and this fit excludes models with p50 > 16h. The 2026-05 Frontier Risk Report calls TH1.1 'essentially saturated'. The strongest shared model had a p50 point estimate of 16–20h and a p80 of 3–4h. At least 16% of successful runs on tasks of 8h or longer were cheating. METR estimates the internal frontier was about 66 days ahead of the public trend, a number it deliberately noised and based on very few models. One METR researcher note shows that model specification alone can move recent models' p50 by -20% to -35% and p80 by up to +100%. For GPT-5.6 Sol, p50 ranges from 11.3h to over 270h depending on how cheating runs are treated, and METR considers none of these robust. The Opus 5.5 evaluation reports no time horizon and describes the model only as 'an incremental improvement above Fable 5.1'.

**口径修正**:
- (b) The 128.7-day doubling time → add 'excludes points with p50 > 16h (Mythos is not in the fit)'; the Opus 4.6 CI upper bound 3634 is from the current METR yaml (Epoch's mirror gives 3950), and numbers shift across versions
- (c) 'Internal frontier leads by about 66 days' → add 'METR deliberately noised this number, it rests on very few models, and it was converted with a 105-day doubling time'
- (c) 16% → 'at least 16% (shared models, tasks of 8h or longer)'
- (d) Attribute it as 'a METR researcher's Note (not METR's official position)'; the 35%/100% figures are verbatim
- (f) Add the third estimate, 71h [13h–11400h], and METR's statement that none of the numbers are robust
- (g) Add that Anthropic could review and edit the text

**核对过程**:I fetched every source with curl and checked the claims verbatim. The core facts are all true. Five places need a qualifier added, so the verdict is CORRECTED rather than HOLDS.

(a) The page says 'This page is no longer actively updated. LAST UPDATED September 8, 2026'. Its update log reads 'May 8th, 2026: Added Claude Mythos Preview (early) and notice that "Measurements above 16 hrs are unreliable with our current task suite."' Matches the claim.

(b) From the yaml: claude_mythos_preview_early_inspect p50 = 1044.78 min (17.41h), CI [508.9, 3304.3]; p80 = 185.9. claude_opus_4_6_inspect p50 = 718.8, CI [316.7, 3633.8]. from_2023_on doubling time = 128.744 days [104.428, 158.012]. Matches the claim. Two notes on version: the yaml comment on doubling time says 'excludes points with central estimate p50 > 16 hrs', so Mythos is not part of the 128.7-day fit. Epoch's mirror of the same Opus 4.6 row gives CI [319.3, 3949.8], so the CI upper bound depends on which snapshot you use. The TH1.1 launch post in January gave 130.8 [107,161] days and Opus 4.5 = 320 min; after the 2026-03-03 regularization fix these are 128.7 days and 293 min. Numbers have shifted between versions.

(c) Quotes found verbatim in the Frontier Risk Report: 'essentially saturated our Time Horizon 1.1 benchmark'; 'the 50% time horizon point estimate of the most capable shared model was between 16 and 20 hours, and the 80% time horizon point estimate was between 3 and 4 hours'; 'at least 16% of successful runs on Time Horizon 1.1 tasks of 8 hours or longer involved cheating'; 'gap between the internally deployed frontier and the public frontier trend is approximately 66 days'; 'Claude Opus 4.6 ... has a time horizon of 17.8 hours on low-messiness tasks and 6.4 hours on high-messiness tasks'. Caveats on the 66 days: the original says 'we have noised the number' and that it rests on 'a small number of models'. It converts a 1.55x ratio using a 105-day public doubling time, which does not match the yaml's 128.7 days. The 16% applies to 'shared models' only.

(d) Note text: 'decreased recent models' 50% time horizon results by up to 20%'; 'reasonable alternative fits decrease the 50% time horizons by up to 35%, and increase the 80% time horizon by up to 100%'. Both are verbatim, so the 100% figure is in the original. However, this is a Note, and the page states notes 'do not necessarily reflect the views of METR as a whole'. The same note says correcting for baseline noise could cut p50 by about 30% (0–60%), and that p50 on private tasks only falls to 7h11m from 11h59m.

(e) The TH1.1 post says 'increased our suite from 170 to 228 tasks' and the long tasks 'from 14 to 31'. Matches. It also says only 5 of the 31 long tasks have human baselines.

(f) Verbatim: 'around 11.3hrs (95% CI: 5hrs - 40hrs), but if we count the cheating attempts as legitimate successes, the point estimate jumps beyond 270hrs'. The same post also gives a third estimate: discarding the cheating runs yields '71hrs (95% CI: 13hrs - 11400hrs)'. METR says it does not consider any of the three numbers a robust measurement.

(g) The Opus 5.5 post gives no time horizon number; its 5 tasks are NanoGPT/LMCA/Train a Program/Gaming Bot/Sunlight. It contains 'Claude Opus 5.5 is an incremental improvement above Fable 5.1 on our quantitative evaluations'. Note that Anthropic had the chance to edit the text ('Anthropic had the opportunity to review and edit the text').

来源:https://metr.org/time-horizons (curl, Updates log); https://metr.org/assets/benchmark_results_1_1.yaml (parsed with pyyaml); https://metr.org/blog/2026-05-19-frontier-risk-report/ ; https://metr.org/notes/2026-03-20-impact-of-modelling-assumptions-on-time-horizon-results/ ; https://metr.org/blog/2026-1-29-time-horizon-1-1/ ; https://metr.org/blog/2026-06-26-gpt-5-6-sol/ ; https://metr.org/blog/2026-09-22-claude-opus-5-5/ ; Epoch benchmark_data.zip metr_time_horizons_external.csv (cross-check)

# G17 — 票型 ['CORRECTED', 'CORRECTED', 'CORRECTED']

## 票 1:CORRECTED(单源已核)

**修正后表述**:On METR TH1.1, the p50 multiples between leading closed models from different labs are: GPT-5 vs Opus 4.1 about 2.0x, GPT-5.2 vs Opus 4.5 about 1.2x, and Opus 4.6 vs Gemini 3.1 Pro about 1.9x. Mythos Preview (a limited preview) is about 2.7x Gemini 3.1 Pro. The CIs overlap for every pair, some only partially. Opus 4.6 and Gemini 3.1 Pro differ by only 1.8pt in average score, yet p50 differs 1.87x and p80 is reversed (0.78x). The p80 CIs overlap almost completely, and the 50/80 thresholds are sensitive to the fitted slope. The p50/p80 ratio has stayed roughly flat at 4–6 since 2023 (Opus 4.6 is an outlier at about 10), so the reliability threshold has not caught up with the 50% threshold. On MirrorCode, METR reported Opus 4.6 >100h and three labs' models all >32h. In Epoch's later version (2026-08/09; mean_score, differing effort levels), Opus 5.5 (max) scores 0.77, Fable 5.1 0.73, GPT-6 Astra 0.47, GPT-5.5 0.10, and Gemini 3.1 Pro (February) 0.09. No open-source model has been evaluated.

**口径修正**:
- (a) The four ratios HOLD. 'Large CI overlap' → change to 'CIs overlap for every pair, but GPT-5 vs Opus 4.1 and Mythos vs Gemini 3.1 Pro overlap only partially'. Mark Mythos as a limited preview model, not a public release.
- (b) The numbers HOLD. 'Ranking reverses with the threshold' → add that the p80 CIs overlap almost completely, so the reversal is not significant. It is also partly a logistic-slope fitting artifact, per METR's 03-20 note.
- (c) The ratios HOLD. 'Reliability has not caught up with capability' → change to 'the p50/p80 ratio has stayed roughly flat at 4–6 since 2023 (Claude 3.5 Sonnet was 6.8–7.9 in 2024), so p80 has not closed the gap with p50, but it is not widening either. Opus 4.6's 10.3 is an outlier.' Do not write it as divergence.
- (d) The numbers HOLD. Add: Opus 5.5 ran at max effort while the others ran at high. Gemini 3.1 Pro is from February. The CSV's mean_score and the risk report's MirrorCode-Early time horizon are different versions and metrics and must not be read as one continuous series.

**核对过程**:I recomputed from the YAML. (a) GPT-5 203.0 / Opus 4.1 100.5 = 2.02. GPT-5.2 352.2 / Opus 4.5 293.0 = 1.20. Opus 4.6 718.8 / Gemini 3.1 Pro 384.1 = 1.87. Mythos 1044.8 / 384.1 = 2.72. All match. CI overlaps: GPT-5 [113,406] vs Opus 4.1 [59,160] overlap only on 113–160. Opus 4.6 [317,3634] vs Gemini [233,695] overlap a lot. Mythos [509,3304] vs Gemini [233,695] overlap only on 509–695. So 'large overlap' does not hold for every pair. The Mythos comparison is also a limited preview model against a public model released 7 weeks earlier. Comparing Opus 4.6 with GPT-5.3-Codex, released the same day (349.5), gives 2.06x. (b) average_score 0.7886 − 0.7703 = +1.83pt. p50 ratio 1.87. p80 69.9 / 89.8 = 0.78. All match. However, the p80 CIs [27,170] and [52,159] overlap almost completely, so the 'ranking reversal' is not statistically significant. The 03-20 note also says logistic fits 'fits shallow slopes that depress 80% time horizons and increase 50% time horizons', so this is partly a fitting artifact. (c) p50/p80 recomputed: GPT-4 4.48, o3 3.99, GPT-5 5.30, Opus 4.5 5.93, Opus 4.6 10.29, Gemini 3.1 Pro 4.28, GPT-5.4 6.34, Mythos 5.62. All match. But the same YAML has Claude 3.5 Sonnet (Jun 2024) at 6.82 and (Oct 2024) at 7.91, o1 at 5.48, and GPT-4o at 5.52. From 2023 to 2026 the ratio stays roughly flat at 4–6. It is not widening, and Opus 4.6's 10.3 is an outlier driven by its very wide p50 CI. The data supports 'p80 has not caught up with p50 (the ratio has not shrunk)'. It does not support 'reliability falling further behind'. (d) Verbatim from the Frontier Risk Report: 'On MirrorCode-Early, Opus 4.6, Gemini 3.1 Pro, and GPT-5.4 all had time horizons greater than 32 hours, and Opus 4.6 had a time horizon greater than 100 hours'. In Epoch's mirrorcode.csv: claude-opus-5-5_max 0.774, claude-fable-5-1_high 0.733, gpt-6-astra_high 0.467, gpt-5.5_high 0.100, gemini-3.1-pro-preview_high 0.0889. All match, and no open-source model appears. Caliber problems: Opus 5.5 ran at 'max' while the others ran at 'high'. stderr is 0.05–0.12. Gemini 3.1 Pro (Feb 2026) is 7 months older than the Sep 2026 models, so it cannot stand in for 'the current Google frontier'. The CSV is a different MirrorCode version (id mirrorcode-ml-2l), and mean_score is not a time horizon: in that report GPT-5.4 and Gemini were >32h, yet here they score only 0.16 and 0.09. The table also omits Opus 4.7 0.311, GPT-5.6 Sol 0.200, and Fable 5 0.639.

## 票 2:CORRECTED(单源已核)

**修正后表述**:On METR's TH1.1, the 50% horizons of the leading closed models differ by about 1.2–2.7x as point estimates. Examples are GPT-5 203 vs Opus 4.1 100 minutes, GPT-5.2 352 vs Opus 4.5 293, Opus 4.6 719 vs Gemini 3.1 Pro 384, and Mythos 1045 vs 384. The confidence intervals overlap heavily, and Opus 4.6's lead is fragile: on private tasks only, its horizon is 7h11m, about 1.1x Gemini 3.1 Pro. Rankings depend on the threshold. Opus 4.6 is 1.8pt ahead on average score and 1.87x on p50, but only 0.78x Gemini 3.1 Pro on p80. The p50/p80 ratio has stayed roughly 4–6x from GPT-4 to Mythos, with Opus 4.6 an outlier at 10.3, so reliability has not narrowed relative to capability. METR itself notes, however, that the logistic fit systematically depresses p80. On MirrorCode-Early, METR measured Opus 4.6 above 100h and all three labs' models above 32h. On Epoch's current MirrorCode as of 2026-09, mean scores were Opus 5.5 (max) 0.77 and Fable 5.1 0.73 (within 1 SE of each other), GPT-6 Astra 0.47, GPT-5.5 0.10 and Gemini 3.1 Pro 0.09. No open-source model has been measured.

**口径修正**:
- (a) The ratios reproduce exactly, but each needs the note that the 95% CIs overlap heavily, so the 1.2–2.7x gaps are not statistically separable.
- (a)(b) Opus 4.6 vs Gemini 3.1 Pro at 1.87x → add that on private tasks only, Opus 4.6's horizon is 7h11m and the ratio is about 1.1x (METR's 03-20 note). The 'flips between thresholds' observation holds.
- (c) The numbers all hold. 'Reliability has not caught up with capability' should be softened to 'the p50/p80 ratio has not narrowed (roughly 4–6x most of the time; Opus 4.6's 10.3 is an outlier)'. Add that METR says the logistic slope systematically depresses p80, so part of the ratio is a modelling artifact.
- (d) MirrorCode >100h / >32h → must be labelled MirrorCode-Early. METR says it does not reflect the current benchmark.
- (d) Epoch mean_score → add stderr (~0.08–0.12); Opus 5.5 vs Fable 5.1 is not distinguishable; reasoning settings differ (max vs high); the metric is mean score, not a time horizon. Change '2026-08/09' to 'as of 2026-09 (entries released 2026-02 to 09)'.

**核对过程**:(a) Recomputed from the yaml: GPT-5 203.0 / Opus 4.1 100.5 = 2.02x. GPT-5.2 352.2 / Opus 4.5 293.0 = 1.20x. Opus 4.6 718.8 / Gemini 3.1 Pro 384.1 = 1.87x. Mythos 1044.8 / 384.1 = 2.72x. CI overlap is large: Opus 4.6 [317, 3634] vs Gemini 3.1 [234, 695]; GPT-5 [113, 406] vs Opus 4.1 [59, 160]. (b) average_score is 0.7886 vs 0.7703, a gap of +1.83pt. The p50 ratio is 1.87x and the p80 ratio is 69.9/89.8 = 0.78x, so the ordering does flip between thresholds. (c) p50/p80 ratios recomputed: GPT-4 4.48, o3 3.99, GPT-5 5.30, Opus 4.5 5.93, Opus 4.6 10.29, Gemini 3.1 Pro 4.28, GPT-5.4 6.34, Mythos 5.62. All match. Contrary readings: (1) The ratio does not trend upward. Gemini 3.1 at 4.3 and Mythos at 5.6 fall back toward the GPT-4 level, and Opus 4.6's 10.3 is a lone outlier. 'Reliability has not caught up' can stand; 'the gap keeps widening' cannot. (2) METR's methods note says the logistic 'fits shallow slopes that depress 80% time horizons and increase 50% time horizons', and alternative fits raise p80 by up to 100% and lower p50 by up to 35%. Part of the p50/p80 ratio is therefore a modelling artifact, and the Opus 4.6 outlier is especially fragile. (3) The same note says Opus 4.6's 50% horizon falls 40% to 7h11m (about 431 minutes) on private tasks only. On that cut the Opus 4.6/Gemini 3.1 ratio drops from 1.87x to about 1.1x, so the 1.9x is not robust. (4) Configurations differ: in Epoch's mirror, gpt-5.4 is xhigh, gpt-5/5.2 are high, and the Opus entries are unknown. (d) Risk Report quote: 'On MirrorCode-Early, Opus 4.6, Gemini 3.1 Pro, and GPT-5.4 all had time horizons greater than 32 hours, and Opus 4.6 had a time horizon greater than 100 hours'. It holds, but the object is MirrorCode-Early, and METR notes 'all information in this report is about an early version of MirrorCode, and does not reflect the current state of the benchmark'. Epoch mirrorcode.csv: claude-opus-5-5_max 0.774 (stderr 0.083), claude-fable-5-1_high 0.733 (0.092), claude-fable-5_high 0.639, gpt-6-astra_high 0.467 (0.119), claude-opus-4-7_high 0.311, gpt-5.6-sol_high 0.200, gpt-5.4_high 0.156, gpt-5.5_high 0.100, gemini-3.1-pro-preview_high 0.089. Nothing from an open-source developer appears. The figures hold, but: Opus 5.5 vs Fable 5.1 is inside 1 SE; Opus 5.5 ran at max while the others ran at high; release dates span 2026-02 to 09, so the file is not a single Aug/Sep cross-section; and the metric is mean_score, not a horizon. Gemini 3.1 Pro shows >32h on MirrorCode-Early but 0.089 on the current version, which shows the two versions cannot be mixed.

来源:https://metr.org/assets/benchmark_results_1_1.yaml (recomputed in Python); https://metr.org/blog/2026-05-19-frontier-risk-report/; https://metr.org/notes/2026-03-20-impact-of-modelling-assumptions-on-time-horizon-results/; https://epoch.ai/data/benchmark_data.zip → mirrorcode.csv

## 票 3:CORRECTED(单源已核)

**修正后表述**:On METR TH1.1 (current yaml), the p50 gap between closed frontier labs releasing at about the same time has ranged from 1.2x to 2x: GPT-5 vs Opus 4.1 is 2.0x, GPT-5.2 vs Opus 4.5 is 1.2x, and Opus 4.6 vs Gemini 3.1 Pro is 1.9x. The confidence intervals overlap substantially in every case. Opus 4.6 vs Gemini 3.1 Pro shows how fragile rankings are: average score differs by only +1.8pt, Opus 4.6 is 1.87x ahead at p50, yet it is only 0.78x Gemini at p80, so the ranking flips with the threshold. Since GPT-4, the p50/p80 ratio has stayed around 4–8x (Opus 4.6's 10.3x is the outlier). In other words, the 80%-reliability horizon has not closed in on the 50% horizon. This ratio is largely set by the slope of the logistic fit, and a METR researcher's note points out that this fit tends to underestimate p80. On METR's early version of MirrorCode, the Opus 4.6, Gemini 3.1 Pro and GPT-5.4 time horizons all exceed 32h, and Opus 4.6's exceeds 100h. Epoch's own MirrorCode version (task completion rate, 2026-08/09) shows a clear spread: Opus 5.5 0.77 and Fable 5.1 0.73 (statistically tied), GPT-6 Astra 0.47, GPT-5.6 Sol 0.20, GPT-5.5 0.10, Gemini 3.1 Pro 0.09. Epoch has not run any open models on it.

**口径修正**:
- (a) The numbers hold; add that same-period pairings can give different ratios (Opus 4.6 vs GPT-5.3-Codex is 2.06x; Gemini 3 Pro / GPT-5.1-Codex-Max / Opus 4.5 are nearly tied), and that Mythos is a preview and above the 16h reliability limit
- (b) HOLDS: +1.83pt / 1.87x / 0.78x recomputed exactly
- (c) 'Reliability has not caught up with capability' → 'p50/p80 has stayed around 4–8x since GPT-4, neither shrinking nor clearly widening; Opus 4.6's 10.3x is an outlier'; also note the ratio is set by the logistic slope, and METR's own note says logistic fits underestimate p80
- (d) Do not put METR's MirrorCode-Early time horizon and Epoch's mirrorcode (ml-2l, mean_score) in one sentence as the same benchmark; Opus 5.5 vs Fable 5.1 differ by less than one stderr, so treat them as tied; add GPT-5.6 Sol 0.20 and Opus 4.7 0.31 to the list; 'no open models' = not tested

**核对过程**:Recomputed from the yaml. (a) GPT-5 203.0 / Opus 4.1 100.5 = 2.02. GPT-5.2 352.2 / Opus 4.5 293.0 = 1.20. Opus 4.6 718.8 / Gemini 3.1 Pro 384.1 = 1.87. Mythos 1044.8 / 384.1 = 2.72. The CIs overlap for every pair, e.g. Mythos [509, 3304] vs Gemini [234, 695], and GPT-5 [113, 406] vs Opus 4.1 [59, 160]. Pairing matters, though. Opus 4.6 vs GPT-5.3-Codex, released the same day at 349.5, gives 2.06x. Gemini 3 Pro 224.3, GPT-5.1-Codex-Max 223.7 and Opus 4.5 293.0 are nearly tied. Mythos is a preview with limited access, and its p50 is above the 16h reliability limit, so comparing it with the public Gemini is not like for like.

(b) average_score 0.7886 − 0.7703 = +1.83 pt. p50 ratio 1.87. p80 69.9 / 89.8 = 0.78. Recomputation matches, and the ranking does flip with the threshold.

(c) p50/p80: GPT-4 4.48, o3 3.99, GPT-5 5.30, Opus 4.5 5.93, Opus 4.6 10.29, Gemini 3.1 Pro 4.28, GPT-5.4 6.34, Mythos 5.62. All match. Other rows for comparison: GPT-4o 5.52, Claude 3.5 Sonnet (Oct) 7.91, o1 5.48, GPT-5.2 5.34. The ratio has stayed between about 4 and 8 from 2023 to 2026. It shows no sustained widening (GPT-4 in 2023 at 4.48 vs Gemini 3.1 Pro in 2026 at 4.28), and Opus 4.6 is a single outlier. More importantly, in a logistic fit the p50/p80 ratio is set entirely by the fitted slope β. The METR note says the logistic model 'fits shallow slopes that depress 80% time horizons and increase 50% time horizons', and that alternative fits can raise p80 by up to 100%. So the ratio is partly a modeling artifact. 'Reliability has not caught up with capability' can only be read as 'p80 grows roughly in step with p50, and the gap is not shrinking'. It cannot be read as 'the gap is widening'.

(d) The Risk Report says, verbatim: 'On MirrorCode-Early, Opus 4.6, Gemini 3.1 Pro, and GPT-5.4 all had time horizons greater than 32 hours, and Opus 4.6 had a time horizon greater than 100 hours'. Epoch's mirrorcode.csv has ids 'mirrorcode-ml-2l-*' and measures mean_score (share of tasks), not time horizon, with runs between 2026-08-10 and 09-22. Opus 5.5 0.774 (stderr 0.083), Fable 5.1 0.733 (0.092), GPT-6 Astra 0.467 (0.119), GPT-5.6 Sol 0.200, GPT-5.5 0.100, Gemini 3.1 Pro 0.089, plus Opus 4.7 0.311, Fable 5 0.639 and GPT-5.4 0.156. All 9 rows are US closed models. These are two benchmark versions with two different metrics. 'Gemini 3.1 Pro >32h' and 'Gemini 3.1 Pro 0.089' cannot be presented side by side as the same benchmark. Opus 5.5 vs Fable 5.1 differ by 0.041, which is inside one stderr, so they are effectively tied. 'No open models' only means Epoch did not run any; it does not mean open models scored 0.

来源:https://metr.org/assets/benchmark_results_1_1.yaml (recomputed ratios); https://metr.org/notes/2026-03-20-impact-of-modelling-assumptions-on-time-horizon-results/ ; https://metr.org/blog/2026-05-19-frontier-risk-report/ (Appendix E MirrorCode); Epoch benchmark_data.zip mirrorcode.csv and benchmark_metadata.csv

# G18 — 票型 ['CORRECTED', 'CORRECTED', 'CORRECTED']

## 票 1:CORRECTED(单源已核)

**修正后表述**:METR's open-source evidence is entirely on TH1.0 and stops at 2025-11. A self-fit of METR's public TH1.0 runs gives open-model lags (when the closed-model envelope first reached the same p50, with log interpolation) of roughly 6.2 months for DeepSeek-V3, 3.4 for R1, 6.8 for R1-0528, 7.2 for gpt-oss-120b, and 8 for Kimi K2 Thinking. A step-function calculation gives 3–7 months, so the lag shows a mild widening at most and depends on the method. Against the best closed model available at each release, the p50 ratio went from about 1.5x for R1 to about 2.8x for R1-0528, then settled around 2.3–2.5x. Kimi K2 Thinking measured about 54 minutes (CI 25–100) through a third-party inference provider, and METR flagged lower confidence in that estimate. The figure of about 3.7x mixes TH1.0 and TH1.1 and should not be used. METR itself wrote in 2025-02 that open-weight models were 'on par with state-of-the-art models from 6 months ago', and in 2025-06 that mid-2025 DeepSeek models matched late-2024 frontier models. METR has never measured an open-source model on TH1.1, so its open-source lag data ends with Kimi K2 Thinking in 2025-11.

**口径修正**:
- (a) The lag figures HOLD, but must be labeled 'self-fit (unpenalized, invsqrt weighting) + log-interpolated envelope'. The step method gives 3.0–6.7 months. Use METR's official 54 minutes (CI 25–100) for Kimi p50, and note that it was measured through a third-party inference provider and METR has lower confidence in it.
- (b) Ruling: 3.7x / 8.4 months is dead, because it compares TH1.0 Kimi 54 with TH1.1 GPT-5 203 across suites. The valid figure is TH1.0 GPT-5 vs Kimi at about 2.3–2.5x (self-fit 130.8/57.6 = 2.27; official 137/54 = 2.53). 'GPT-5 TH1.0 about 131' → the official value is about 137 (2h17m); 131 is a self-fit.
- (c) HOLDS. The 6-month sentence is about 'open-weight models' as a group, not DeepSeek-V3 alone.
- (d) HOLDS. Add that this means METR's open-source lag data stops at 2025-11 and cannot describe the gap in 2026.

**核对过程**:Method: for each model in the TH1.0 runs, I fit a maximum-likelihood logistic of score_binarized on log2(human_minutes), weighted by invsqrt_task_weight with no penalty. I took the running maximum over closed models as the envelope. For each open model I found when the envelope reached its p50 and measured the lag to the open model's release. (a) With log-linear interpolation along the envelope, the lags are V3 6.2, R1 3.4, V3-0324 6.0, R1-0528 6.8, gpt-oss-120b 7.2, and Kimi K2 Thinking 8.1 months. This reproduces the claim (8.1 vs 8.2 is within noise). With a step function (the first closed model that actually reached that level), the lags are 6.2 / 3.0 / 5.0 / 5.7 / 5.3 / 6.7 months. The 'lag' therefore moves by 1–2 months depending on the method, which needs to be stated. My self-fit Kimi p50 is 57.6 minutes; METR's official figure is 54 (CI 25–100). At-release ratios (against the best closed model released no later than the open model): R1 1.49x (o1), R1-0528 2.79x (o3), gpt-oss 2.42x (Opus 4.1), Kimi 2.27x (GPT-5 self-fit 130.8). This matches '1.5 → 2.8 → 2.3–2.4x'. (b) In Epoch's mirror, the kimi-k2-thinking row has an empty version field, so it is TH1.0. gpt-5_high 203.0 is METR-Horizon-v1.1. 54/203 = 0.27 (3.7x) mixes the two suites and is invalid. The same-suite comparison is TH1.0 GPT-5: official 137.3 min (Epoch row gpt-5 medium, 2h17m) or self-fit 130.8. That gives 2.5x (official numbers) or 2.3x (self-fit). The '131' is a self-fit value, not an official one. The 8.4-month lag computed from the mixed Epoch mirror is also invalid. (c) Verbatim from the 2025-02-12 DeepSeek-V3 report: 'the performance of open-weight models on our tasks is on par with state-of-the-art models from 6 months ago'. The subject is open-weight models as a whole. Verbatim from the 2025-06-27 report: 'the level of autonomous capabilities of mid-2025 DeepSeek models is similar to the level of capabilities of frontier models from late 2024'. Both match. (d) The TH1.1 runs.jsonl and YAML contain only Anthropic, OpenAI and Google models, with no open-source model. The latest commit is 2026-03-06. The time-horizons update log: 'November 19th, 2025: Added GPT-5.1-Codex-Max and Kimi K2 Thinking'; no open-source model appears after that. METR's X post states that the Kimi evaluation was 'through a third-party inference provider, which reduces our confidence in this estimate'.

来源:https://raw.githubusercontent.com/METR/eval-analysis-public/main/reports/time-horizon-1-0/data/raw/runs.jsonl (my own fit); .../time-horizon-1-1/data/raw/runs.jsonl (model list); Epoch metr_time_horizons_external.csv; https://metr.org/evaluations/deepseek-v3-report/; https://metr.org/evaluations/deepseek-qwen-report/; https://metr.org/time-horizons/ update log; METR X post on Kimi K2 Thinking

## 票 2:CORRECTED(方向存争)

**修正后表述**:Refitting METR's TH1.0 raw runs (weighted logistic, interpolated on the running-max envelope of closed models) gives open-model lags behind the closed frontier of about 6.2 months for DeepSeek-V3, 3.4 for R1, 6.8 for R1-0528, 7.2 for gpt-oss-120b and 8.2 for Kimi K2 Thinking (2025-11). With a step rule instead of interpolation, the figures are 3.0–6.7 months. At release, the best closed model's 50% horizon was 1.5x R1's, 2.8x R1-0528's and about 2.3–2.5x Kimi K2 Thinking's (GPT-5 about 131–137 vs Kimi about 54–58 minutes). Any '3.7x' figure mixes the TH1.0 and TH1.1 versions and is invalid. METR's own reports at the time put DeepSeek-V3 'on par with state-of-the-art models from 6 months ago' and R1 at 7 months behind. They also said mid-2025 DeepSeek models were similar to 'frontier models from late 2024'. METR has never measured an open-weight model on TH1.1; the last one was Kimi K2 Thinking, added on 2025-11-19. On a different scale, the independent CAISI evaluation in 2026-04/05 found DeepSeek V4 Pro about 8 months behind the US frontier.

**口径修正**:
- (a) Lags 6.2/3.4/6.8/7.2/8.2 months hold only under the interpolated running-max envelope. Under the step rule they are 3.4/3.0/5.7/5.3/6.7. The method must be stated, or both given as a range.
- (a) Release-time ratios 1.5x → 2.8x → 2.3–2.4x hold on the refit. With METR's published TH1.0 values, Kimi is 137.3/54.2 = 2.5x, so write about 2.3–2.5x.
- (b) Ruling: 判死: 'Kimi K2 Thinking 54 vs GPT-5 high 203 → 0.27 (about 3.7x)' mixes TH1.0 and TH1.1. Use about 2.3–2.5x within TH1.0, with the closed baseline taken as the best closed model released on or before the open model's date. The catch-up time of about 8.2–8.4 months can stay.
- (b) 'GPT-5 TH1.0 约 131 分钟' is my refit value. METR/Epoch publish 137.3 minutes (medium), so cite the source when writing.
- (c) Both quotes are verbatim. Add the contrary evidence: METR's R1 report said 'on-par with state-of-the-art models from 7 months ago', far from the self-computed R1 lag of 3.4 months. METR's contemporaneous verdicts and the retrospective refit use different measures and should not be strung together as one series.
- (d) Holds. You may add CAISI's 2026-05 measurement of DeepSeek V4 Pro as an independent check on whether the 'lag of about 8 months' still held in 2026. Its scale is different and should be labelled.

**核对过程**:Recomputation method: for each model I fit a logistic of score_binarized on log2(human_minutes), weighted by invsqrt_task_weight. p50 = 2^(-b0/b1). L2 strength C = 1e9, 10 and 1 gives identical results. The closed models' running-max p50 forms the envelope, and lag = open model's release date minus the date the envelope reaches its p50, interpolated in log space. Result: V3 6.2, R1 3.4, V3-0324 6.1, R1-0528 6.8, gpt-oss-120b 7.2, Kimi K2 Thinking 8.2 months (Kimi p50 57.6, GPT-5 130.8). This reproduces the claimed numbers exactly. Method sensitivity: using the step rule (date of the first closed model with p50 at or above the open model's) instead gives V3 3.4, R1 3.0, R1-0528 5.7, gpt-oss 5.3, Kimi 6.7 months. Lags are therefore method-dependent by about 1.5–3 months, and the method must be stated. Ratios at release (best closed model released on or before the open model's date): R1 1.49x (o1), R1-0528 2.79x (o3), gpt-oss 2.42x (Opus 4.1), Kimi 2.27x (GPT-5). These reproduce. Ruling on (b): Epoch's METR mirror has a blank-version (TH1.0-family) row with kimi-k2-thinking 54.2 and gpt-5_medium 137.3, while GPT-5 high at 203.0 is a METR-Horizon-v1.1 value. The 0.27 (3.7x) therefore divides a TH1.0 Kimi figure by a TH1.1 GPT-5 figure and is invalid. Comparing within TH1.0 gives 2.27x on my refit and 137.3/54.2 = 2.53x on METR's published values. The correct range is about 2.3–2.5x. A catch-up time of about 8.2–8.4 months is consistent across both lines. Note that my GPT-5 TH1.0 refit is 130.8 while METR/Epoch publish 137.3 (medium), so '约 131' comes from the refit, not METR's official number. (c) Quotes check: the V3 report (2025-02-12) says 'the performance of open-weight models on our tasks is on par with state-of-the-art models from 6 months ago'. The 2025-06-27 report says 'the level of autonomous capabilities of mid-2025 DeepSeek models is similar to the level of capabilities of frontier models from late 2024'. Contrary evidence: the R1 report (2025-03-05) says 'on-par with state–of-the-art models from 7 months ago'. My recomputed R1 lag is 3.4 months, and the step method gives 3.0, so METR's own contemporaneous verdict disagrees with the self-computed figure by a factor of about 2. METR's early reports were based on general-autonomy tasks and RE-Bench, not today's TH1.0 refit, which is why they differ. (d) TH1.1 yaml contains only closed models. The last open-model entry in the update log is 'November 19th, 2025: Added ... Kimi K2 Thinking'. The Frontier Risk Report evaluates no open-weight model and mentions DeepSeek only via CAISI's PortBench. This holds. Independent corroboration of the size of the lag: CAISI (NIST, 2026-05-01) says 'DeepSeek V4's capabilities lag behind the frontier by about 8 months' and 'performs similarly to GPT-5, which was released about 8 months ago'. That is a different scale (IRT aggregate of nine benchmarks, two of them private), but it agrees with the 8.2 months for Kimi and extends the series to 2026-04.

来源:https://raw.githubusercontent.com/METR/eval-analysis-public/main/reports/time-horizon-1-0/data/raw/runs.jsonl (41,629 lines, my own refit); https://metr.org/evaluations/deepseek-v3-report/; https://metr.org/evaluations/deepseek-r1-report/; https://metr.org/evaluations/deepseek-qwen-report/; https://metr.org/time-horizons/ update log; benchmark_results_1_1.yaml; Epoch benchmark_data.zip → metr_time_horizons_external.csv; https://www.nist.gov/news-events/news/2026/05/caisi-evaluation-deepseek-v4-pro

## 票 3:CORRECTED(单源已核)

**修正后表述**:Using METR's TH1.0 data (official fits after the regularization fix, log-linear interpolation on the running-max envelope of closed models), open models trailed the closed frontier by: DeepSeek-V3 about 6 months, R1 about 3.4, R1-0528 about 6.8, gpt-oss-120b about 7.2, and Kimi K2 Thinking (p50 about 58 min) about 8 months. Against the best closed model available on its release day, the ratio went from about 1.5x for R1 to about 2.8x for R1-0528, then settled at about 2.3–2.4x. With a different interpolation choice the lag shrinks to 3–7 months, so the direction (no catching up, the gap widening slightly) is more robust than the exact month counts. The '3.7x' figure mixes TH1.0 with TH1.1 and should not be used. METR's own conclusions follow the same line: in 2025-02 it said open-weight models were on par with the frontier of 6 months earlier, and in 2025-06 it said mid-2025 DeepSeek models were at the level of the late-2024 frontier. METR has never published a TH1.1 measurement of any open model. The last one was Kimi K2 Thinking, added in 2025-11, so after 2026 METR data cannot say whether open models are converging on the closed frontier.

**口径修正**:
- (a) The lags (6.2/3.4/6.8/7.2/8.2 months) and ratios (1.5→2.8→2.3–2.4x) recompute exactly; must state 'TH1.0, log-linear interpolation on the closed running-max envelope'; without interpolation they become 3.0–6.7 months; gpt-oss's 2.4x is against the same-day Opus 4.1 (2.9x against GPT-5 two days later); Kimi p50 is officially 57.6 min post-fix (54.2 legacy)
- (b) Ruled out: 'Kimi 54 vs GPT-5 203 = 0.27 (3.7x)' — it mixes TH1.0 (legacy) with TH1.1 and is invalid; the consistent figure is TH1.0 57.6 vs 130.8 = 2.3x (or 2.5x within Epoch's legacy column); the open-vs-closed gap on TH1.1 is unknown
- (c) HOLDS; note the 'six months' sentence is about open-weight models as a group, tested on a third-party-hosted model
- (d) HOLDS; 'last open model 2025-11-19' → 'Kimi K2 Thinking (released 2025-11-06, added to METR's page 2025-11-19)'

**核对过程**:(a) Method: I used METR's official TH1.0 yaml rather than refitting the raw runs. The closed-model frontier is the running max of p50 by release date. Lag = open model release date − the date that envelope first reached the open model's p50, with log-linear interpolation between adjacent envelope points. Results: DeepSeek-V3 6.2 months, R1 3.4, V3-0324 6.1, R1-0528 6.8, gpt-oss-120b 7.2, Kimi K2 Thinking 8.2. These match the claim exactly. Ratio at release vs the best closed model released no later than the open one: V3 2.10x (o1), R1 1.49x, R1-0528 2.79x (o3), gpt-oss 2.42x (Opus 4.1 on the same day; 2.9x against GPT-5 two days later), Kimi 2.27x (GPT-5 at 130.8). Kimi's official TH1.0 p50 is 57.6 min, and the Epoch legacy value is 54.2. Method sensitivity: without interpolation (lag = time since the first closed model with p50 ≥ the open model's), the lags shrink to 3.4/3.0/5.0/5.7/5.3/6.7 months. So the 'months behind' figure depends on the interpolation choice.

(b) In Epoch's mirror, the Kimi K2 Thinking row (54.19) has an empty METR version, which means legacy TH1.0. The gpt-5_high 203.01 row is tagged METR-Horizon-v1.1. 54/203 = 0.27 (3.7x) therefore mixes two versions and is invalid. The consistent TH1.0 comparison is 57.6 vs 130.8 = 2.27x. In Epoch's legacy column, GPT-5 medium 137.3 / 54.2 = 2.53x. GPT-5 on TH1.0 is currently 130.8 min (about 131, as claimed); the January TH1.1 post's table listed TH1 GPT-5 as 138, before the fix. TH1.1 re-estimates moved the closed models up substantially (GPT-5 +55%), and no open model was re-measured, so the open-vs-closed gap on TH1.1 cannot be determined. The adjudication rule proposed in the claim is correct: use the best closed model released no later than the open model, on the same TH version.

(c) Verbatim: DeepSeek-V3 report (DATE February 12, 2025): 'the performance of open-weight models on our tasks is on par with state-of-the-art models from 6 months ago, whereas they had previously lagged significantly further behind'. DeepSeek-Qwen report (DATE June 27, 2025): 'the level of autonomous capabilities of mid-2025 DeepSeek models is similar to the level of capabilities of frontier models from late 2024'. Both hold. The first sentence is about 'open-weight models' in general, not DeepSeek-V3 alone, and METR evaluated it on a model hosted by a third party.

(d) The TH1.1 yaml contains only closed models. The page's update log lists the last open model as 'November 19th, 2025: Added GPT-5.1-Codex-Max and Kimi K2 Thinking' (Kimi was released 2025-11-06; 11-19 is the date it was added to the page). The Frontier Risk Report contains no Kimi, Qwen, GLM, open-weight or gpt-oss; DeepSeek appears only in a citation of the CAISI DeepSeek V4 Pro evaluation. A web search found no METR TH1.1 measurement of any open model. Holds, but should be scoped to 'METR's public outputs'.

来源:https://metr.org/assets/benchmark_results_1_0.yaml (post-regularization-fix TH1.0 official fits, recomputed lags); https://metr.org/assets/benchmark_results_1_1.yaml ; Epoch benchmark_data.zip metr_time_horizons_external.csv ('METR version' column); https://metr.org/blog/2026-1-29-time-horizon-1-1/ ; https://metr.org/evaluations/deepseek-v3-report/ ; https://metr.org/evaluations/deepseek-qwen-report/ ; https://metr.org/time-horizons (Updates log); Frontier Risk Report full text grep

# G19 — 票型 ['CORRECTED', 'CORRECTED', 'CORRECTED']

## 票 1:CORRECTED(单源已核)

**修正后表述**:Terminal-Bench 3.0 公告称旧版'Many Terminal-Bench tasks have become saturated and leaderboard entries are condensed into a narrow range that may not fully represent capability gaps across models';同样六个闭源模型(均为官方 harness),TB2.1 挤在 74.6–83.8%,TB3.0 拉开到 14.3–33.8%(榜首 GPT-5.6 Sol 34.4%);Fable 5 与 Opus 4.8 的差距从 4.9 分扩大到 12.7 分;GLM 5.2(Claude Code harness)只有 5.1%。TB4.0 公开榜(2026-10-06,每个条目 330 trials)上,Opus 5.5 max 为 64.8±3.1%,开放权重最佳 GLM-5.3 为 41.8±3.2%;Anthropic 自报的 66.4% 是它自己 setup 下的 xhigh 档,与公开榜不矛盾,但配置不同。同 harness(Terminus 2)的 TB2.0 回溯数据中,按模型发布月份,闭源与开源最佳分别为 2025-08 的 38.0 vs 27.8、2025-11 的 57.8 vs 35.7、2026-02 的 64.7 vs 约 52;混合 harness 下榜首为 GPT-5.5+NexAU-AHE 84.7%。APEX-Agents(Epoch,2026-09)上 Gemini 4 Argon 82.2%、Sonnet 5.5 75.5%,最佳开放权重模型为 MiMo-V2.6-Pro 59.5%,GLM-5.3 为 56.6%。OSWorld 2 上 Opus 5 (max) binary 31.4%,较早一批开放模型(Kimi K2.6、MiniMax-M3)为 4.6%,但工具设置不同,partial score 的差距约 3x。Vending-Bench 2(Andon Labs,仅计入盈利模型、线性外推):'Chinese lags by ~111 days',中国 SOTA 斜率 +$1,047/月 vs 西方 +$822/月,预计 2027-10 交叉;GPT-6 Astra $15,515,GLM-5.2 $8,314。

**口径修正**:
- TB3 引语:截断处须加省略号,或补全为'...that may not fully represent capability gaps across models'
- TB3.0 区间:'六个闭源 14.3–34.4' → '同六个模型在 TB3.0 为 14.3–33.8%(榜首 GPT-5.6 Sol 34.4% 无 TB2.1 对照)'
- TB4 自报一致性:'与 Anthropic 自报 66.4% 一致' → '66.4% 是 Anthropic 自己的 setup、xhigh 档(SE ±2.6);公开榜只有 max 档 64.8±3.1,配置不同,但在噪声范围内不矛盾'
- TB2.0 时间轴:'2025-08' 须注明是按模型发布月份的回溯评测(TB2.0 于 2025-11 发布);差距 10.2→22.1→12.3 不单调;GLM-5 原始 result.json 复算为 51.9,Epoch 为 52.4
- 判死:APEX-Agents '最佳开源 GLM-5.3-Flash 52.8' → 最佳开放权重模型为 MiMo-V2.6-Pro 59.5(若把基于开放权重 Qwen3.8-2.4T 的 Qwen3.8-Max 算进去则是 63.3),GLM-5.3 为 56.6;闭源对开源的差距约 19–23 分,不是 29.4 分
- OSWorld 2 的 6.8x:须注明两边工具设置不同(batch tool vs standard)、开源侧只有 4–6 月的旧模型、binary 指标低基数;按 partial score 只有约 3.1x
- Vending-Bench 2:须注明'仅计入盈利模型、线性外推'

**核对过程**:(a) TB3 公告逐字:'Many Terminal-Bench tasks have become saturated and leaderboard entries are condensed into a narrow range that may not fully represent capability gaps across models.'(引语被截断,后半句须保留或用省略号)。'Fable 5 in Claude Code and Opus 4.8 in Claude Code are separated by only 4.9 points on Terminal-Bench 2.1, but 12.7 points on Terminal-Bench 3.0' 逐字一致。对比图六个模型 TB2.1:Fable5 83.8/Opus4.8 78.9/Terra 78.4/Grok4.5 79.3/Sonnet5 74.6/Luna 75.7 → 74.6–83.8 对;同六个在 TB3.0 为 33.8/21.1/20.8/17.8/14.6/14.3 → 14.3–33.8。34.4 是 GPT-5.6 Sol,它没有 TB2.1 分数,不在这组六个里。GLM 5.2 (Claude Code) 5.1% 对。(b) TB4.0 榜 JSON:Opus 5.5 max 64.85±3.11、Sonnet 5.5 61.82、GPT-6 Astra max 58.18、GPT-6.1 Sol 58.18、Fable 5.1 max/xhigh 57.88、GLM-5.3 41.82±3.23、Grok 4.7 37.58、Qwen3.8-Max-0902 26.97,n_trials 全部 330,updated 2026-10-06,全部对。Anthropic 页原文:'Terminal-Bench 4.0 results are reported for Claude Opus 5.5 at xhigh effort ... The standard error is ±2.6 pts';66.4% 是 xhigh 档、Anthropic 自己的 setup。公开榜只有 Opus 5.5 max 档(64.8),没有 xhigh 行,所以两者不是同一配置;落在噪声范围内,只能说'不矛盾'。另外 Anthropic 自报 Fable 5.1 为 55.8,榜上是 57.9。GLM、Qwen 在榜上都用 Claude Code harness。(c) Epoch terminalbench_external 的 Terminus 2 行:2025-08 前发布的最佳闭源是 Opus 4.1 38.0(GPT-5 35.2),最佳开源 Kimi-K2-Instruct 27.8;2025-11 是 Opus 4.5 57.8 vs Kimi K2 Thinking 35.7;2026-02 是 GPT-5.3-Codex 64.7 vs GLM-5 52.4(HF 原始 result.json 复算 GLM-5 为 51.9=231/445,Opus 4.6 两次运行合并约 62.9)。三组都复算成立。口径注意:TB2.0 在 2025-11 才发布,'2025-08'是按模型发布月份的回溯评测,不是当时的榜。差距依次是 10.2→22.1→12.3 分,并不单调;2026-02 之后没有开源模型的 Terminus 2 条目,序列到此为止。NexAU-AHE+GPT-5.5 在 Epoch 为 0.847191,对;HF 四个 run 分别为 83.1/85.4/84.8/84.3。(d) Epoch APEX-Agents:Gemini 4 Argon 82.2、Sonnet 5.5 max 75.5、Opus 5.5 max 73.5、GLM-5.3-Flash 52.8、Kimi K3 50.6,数字都对。但'最佳开源=GLM-5.3-Flash 52.8'判死:mimo-v2.6-pro 59.5(XiaomiMiMo/MiMo-V2.6-Pro-RL 于 2026-09-21 在 HF 上线,MIT 许可,不 gated,约 1.02T 参数)更高;GLM-5.3 为 56.6,NIST 原文'Z.ai publicly released the model's weights two weeks later',即约 8 月底开放权重(Epoch 仍标 API access,已过时);qwen3.8-max 63.3,HF 上 Qwen3.8-2.4T-A95B 的 README 写明'Qwen3.8-Max is the official version based on Qwen3.8-2.4T-A95B'(2026-08-08 开放权重,qwen3.8-max 定制许可)。(e) Epoch osworld_2:claude-opus-5_max binary 0.3143(batch tool,500 步);MiniMax-M3 与 kimi-k2.6 为 0.046(standard 工具设置)→ 6.83x,对。但两边工具设置不同(batch vs standard);开源一侧只有 4–6 月的旧模型(Kimi K2.6、MiniMax-M3、qwen3.7-plus),没测 GLM-5.x、Kimi K3、Qwen3.8、MiMo-V2.6;按 partial score 算是 68.3 vs 22.3,约 3.1x。在 4.6% 的低基数上算比值会放大差距。老 OSWorld:Sonnet 4.6 72.1、Kimi K2.5 63.3(来源 OS World Website),对,但那是 2026-02 前后的旧快照。(f) andonlabs 页逐字:'Chinese: +$1,047/month (R² = 0.98) · Western: +$822/month (R² = 0.95) · Chinese lags by ~111 days · Projected crossover: Oct 2027',并注明'Only profitable models are included';榜上 GPT-6 Astra '$15,514.70 ± $1,074';Epoch 中 GLM-5.2 为 8313.78。全部对;crossover 是线性外推。

## 票 2:CORRECTED(单源已核)

**修正后表述**:Terminal-Bench 官方在 3.0 公告中承认老版本“很多任务已饱和、榜单挤在窄区间”:Fable 5 与 Opus 4.8 在 TB2.1 只差 4.9 分,在 TB3.0(74 题)差 12.7 分;有对照的六个闭源模型在 TB2.1 处于 74.6–83.8,在 TB3.0 拉开到 14.3–33.8(GPT-5.6 Sol 34.4% 居首),GLM 5.2 为 5.1%。TB4.0 公开榜(2026-10-06 更新,每模型 330 trials)上,Opus 5.5 (max) 64.8±3.1、GPT-6 Astra/GPT-6.1 Sol 58.2、开放权重的 GLM-5.3 41.8±3.2;Anthropic 自报的 66.4% 是 xhigh 档,与榜单在误差内一致。同 harness(Terminus 2)的 TB2.0 闭源−开源差距 2025-08 为 10.2 分、2025-11 为 22.1 分、2026-02 为 12.3 分,先扩后缩。Epoch 转录的 APEX-Agents 上,Gemini 4 Argon 82.2,最佳开源 MiMo-V2.6-Pro 59.5、GLM-5.3 56.6。OSWorld 2 上 Opus 5 (max, batch tool) 31.4% 对已测开源最高 4.6%;同 standard 设置下约 4 倍,且开源只测到 2026 年中以前的模型。Vending-Bench 2 上,Andon Labs 回归给出“中国落后约 111 天、2027-10 交叉”,但中国 SOTA(GLM-5.2 $8,314)自 6 月以来没有提升,GPT-6 Astra 已到 $15,515,按当前时点实际滞后约 8 个月。

**口径修正**:
- TB3.0「六个闭源 14.3–34.4」→ 同一组六个(有 TB2.1 对照)为 14.3–33.8;34.4 属 GPT-5.6 Sol,它没有 TB2.1 分数。另须注明 TB3.0 只有 74 题、harness 混用;公告日期 2026-07-30 在页面上未能核到
- TB4.0 数字全部吻合;与 Anthropic 自报 66.4% 是一致的,但口径不同:66.4 是 xhigh 档、厂商自跑(±2.6),榜单 64.8 是 max 档、330 trials(±3.1);GLM-5.3、Qwen3.8-Max 都借用 Claude Code harness 跑
- TB2.0 Terminus 2 序列复算一致,但须注明数据止于 2026-02;差距 10.2→22.1→12.3 是先扩后缩,不能写成单调分化
- 判死:APEX-Agents「最佳开源 GLM-5.3-Flash 52.8」→ 最佳开源应为 MiMo-V2.6-Pro 59.5(MIT 开源),其次 GLM-5.3 56.6(NIST 确认已开放权重);与 Gemini 4 Argon 82.2 的差距约 22.7 分,不是 29.4
- OSWorld 2「6.8x」→ 注明闭源用 batch tool、开源用 standard,且开源只测到 2026-06 之前的模型;同 standard 设置约 4x(Opus 4.8 18.5 vs 4.6)。4.6% 绝对值极低,比值噪声大
- Vending-Bench 2「中国落后约 111 天、2027-10 交叉」→ 这是 Andon Labs 的回归口径;按累计 SOTA 复算,中国 SOTA 自 2026-06 起停滞,今天实际滞后约 8 个月,交叉外推与近 4 个月数据相反。另须注明该图按中/西方划分,不是开源/闭源

"reported for Claude Opus 5.5 at xhigh effort",标准误 ±2.6;公开榜是 max 档 64.8±3.1,两者在噪声内一致,但档位不同。公告中 GPT-6 Astra 57.9% 是 high 档、"as reported by OpenAI"。(c) 用 Epoch terminalbench_external.csv 筛 apex_agents_external.csv:Gemini 4 Argon 82.2(effort 未知,SE 4.4)、Sonnet 5.5 75.5、Opus 5.5 73.5,吻合。但「最佳开源 GLM-5.3-Flash 52.8」站不住:同表 MiMo-V2.6-Pro 59.5(2026-09-21 发布,MIT 开源权重,多源报道)、GLM-5.3 56.6(NIST 原文:"Z.ai publicly released the model's weights two weeks later";Epoch 元数据把它标成 API access 是滞后的)都高于 52.8;Kimi K3 50.6 吻合,但它是 non-commercial 开源。(e) osworld_2_external.csv:Claude Opus 5 (max) 31.43,Tool setting 为 batch tool;开源最高 MiniMax-M3 / Kimi K2.6 4.6,setting 为 standard;31.43/4.6=6.83x,算术对。但工具设置不同,年代也不同(开源只测到 2026-04~06 的模型,没测 Kimi K3、GLM-5.3、MiMo-V2.6)。同 standard 设置下最强闭源是 Opus 4.8 max 18.5,比值约 ## 票 3:CORRECTED(单源已核)

**修正后表述**:Terminal-Bench 团队在 TB3.0 公告中承认旧任务已饱和、榜单『挤在很窄的区间』:同一组六个闭源模型在 TB2.1 上分布于 74.6–83.8,在 TB3.0 上拉开到 14.3–33.8(Fable 5 与 Opus 4.8 的差距从 4.9 分扩大到 12.7 分;各模型用各自厂商 harness;GLM 5.2 在 Claude Code harness 下仅 5.1%)。TB4.0 公开榜(2026-10-06,每模型 330 trials)上,Opus 5.5 (max) 64.8±3.1,与 Anthropic 自报的 66.4%(xhigh、自家 setup,SE ±2.6)在误差内一致;GLM-5.3(8 月底已开放权重)41.8±3.2,闭源的 Qwen3.8-Max 27.0。同 harness(Terminus 2)下 TB2.0 闭源与开源前沿差距为 2025-08 38.0 vs 27.8、2025-11 57.8 vs 35.7、2026-02 64.7 vs 52.4,先扩后缩。APEX-Agents 上 2026-09 闭源最高 Gemini 4 Argon 82.2,开放权重最高 MiMo-V2.6-Pro 59.5、GLM-5.3 56.6。OSWorld 2 上 Opus 5 (max) 31.4% 对开源最好 4.6%,但两边工具设置不同、开源只测了较老的模型,同条件比约 3–4 倍。Vending-Bench 2 中国 SOTA 拟合线落后约 111 天(按原始 SOTA 现约 6 个月),2027-10 交叉只是线性外推。

**口径修正**:
- (a) TB3.0 区间『14.3–34.4』改为『同组六个闭源 TB2.1 74.6–83.8 → TB3.0 14.3–33.8(加上不在 TB2.1 对比组的 GPT-5.6 Sol 34.4,最高为 34.4)』;要注明各家用各自 harness(Claude Code/Codex/Cursor CLI),GLM 5.2 跑在 Claude Code harness 里;公告日期 7-30 未在页面上核实到
- (b) 榜单数字全对;要注明 GLM-5.3 自 8/28 起开放权重,Qwen3.8-Max 是闭源 API 模型(只能算『中国』不能算『开源』);Anthropic 自报 66.4% 是 xhigh、自家 setup,公开榜 64.8±3.1 是 max、Claude Code,差 1.6,在 SE ±2.6 以内,算一致,不算矛盾
- (c) 三个时点的 Terminus 2 数字都复现了,但要补一句:差距是 10.2→22.1→12.3,不是单调扩大;NexAU-AHE 是第三方 agent(组织 china-qijizhifeng);Epoch TB2.0 转录缺 2026-03 之后的开源条目
- 判死:(d)『最佳开源 GLM-5.3-Flash 52.8』→ 改为『最佳开放权重 MiMo-V2.6-Pro 59.5,其次 GLM-5.3 56.6、GLM-5.3-Flash 52.8、Kimi K3 50.6(非商用许可)』;Gemini 4 Argon 对最佳开源的差距从 29.4 分改为 22.7 分
- (e) 6.8x 算术无误,但要改写为『Opus 5 (max, batch tool) 31.4% 对开源 4.6%(standard 设置、4–6 月老模型,K3/GLM-5.x 未测);同设置同期比约 3–4x』
- (f) 引语和数字都对;『Chinese』要写成『中国 SOTA(含闭源)』;111 天是趋势线时移,按原始 SOTA 现在的滞后约 6 个月;2027-10 交叉是线性外推,要标为预测

**核对过程**:(a) TB3.0 公告原文逐字有 'Many Terminal-Bench tasks have become saturated and leaderboard entries are condensed into a narrow range that may not fully represent capability gaps across models.' 原文还写 'Fable 5 in Claude Code and Opus 4.8 in Claude Code are separated by only 4.9 points on Terminal-Bench 2.1, but 12.7 points on Terminal-Bench 3.0'(83.8-78.9=4.9;33.8-21.1=12.7,复算无误)。对比图里有六个模型:TB2.1 是 Fable5 83.8 / Opus4.8 78.9 / GPT-5.6 Terra 78.4 / Grok4.5 79.3 / Sonnet5 74.6 / Luna 75.7,同一组在 TB3.0 是 33.8/21.1/20.8/17.8/14.6/14.3。所以同一组六个的 TB3.0 区间是 14.3–33.8。34.4 是 GPT-5.6 Sol,它不在 TB2.1 对比组里,原表述把两个集合混在了一起。GLM 5.2 的 5.1% 无误,但它跑在 Claude Code harness 里,而 GPT 跑在 Codex、Grok 跑在 Cursor CLI 里,每家 harness 都不一样。页面上没找到 2026-07-30 这个日期字样,日期未能核实。(b) 我从 TB4.0 榜单内嵌 JSON 读出:Opus 5.5 64.85 ±3.11,n_trials 330,effort max,Claude Code;Sonnet 5.5 61.82;GPT-6 Astra 58.18(max,Codex);GPT-6.1 Sol 58.18;Fable 5.1 57.88(max);GLM-5.3 41.82 ±3.23(Claude Code,max);Grok 4.7 37.58(Grok Build,xhigh);Qwen3.8-Max-0902 26.97。全部吻合,updated_at 是 2026-10-06。Anthropic 原文表格里 TB4.0 是 66.4%,脚注写 'Terminal-Bench 4.0 results are reported for Claude Opus 5.5 at xhigh effort',又写 'The standard error is ±2.6 pts for Claude Opus 5.5',还写公开榜 'reports Claude Opus 5 at 51.8%; our setup reproduces it at 52.3%, within noise'。66.4(xhigh,厂商自己的 setup)和 64.8(max,公开榜)差 1.6 分,在 1 个 SE 以内,可以说一致,但档位和 setup 都不同。另外,Epoch 元数据把 GLM-5.3 标为 API access,这个标注已过时:NIST 9/17 原文写 'Z.ai publicly released the model's weights two weeks later',所以 GLM-5.3 现在算开放权重。Qwen3.8-Max-0902 是闭源,只有 API(Epoch 标注),只能算『中国』,不能算『开源』。(c) 我用 Epoch terminalbench_external.csv 里 Agent=Terminus 2 的行,结合 model_metadata 的 accessibility,按日期累积取前沿复算:截至 2025-08 闭源 Opus 4.1 38.0,开源 Kimi K2 27.8(7 月发布);截至 2025-11 闭源 Opus 4.5 57.8,开源 Kimi K2 Thinking 35.7;截至 2026-02 闭源 GPT-5.3-Codex 64.7,开源 GLM-5 52.4。全部复现。但差距是 10.2→22.1→12.3,先扩后缩,不是单调走势。混合 harness 下 GPT-5.5+NexAU-AHE 84.7 无误,但做这个 agent 的组织是 china-qijizhifeng,并不是闭源厂商自己。而且 Epoch 的 TB2.0 转录缺 2026-03 之后的开源条目(GLM-5.1、Kimi K2.6、DeepSeek V4 都没有),混合 harness 比较的时间不对齐。(d) APEX-Agents(Epoch 转录)里 Gemini 4 Argon 82.2(effort 未知,9/30 发布)、Sonnet 5.5 75.5、Opus 5.5 73.5(max)、GLM-5.3-Flash 52.8、Kimi K3 50.6,数字都对,但『最佳开源』这个说法站不住:MiMo-V2.6-Pro 59.5(Xiaomi,9/21 以 MIT 许可开放权重,多方报道)和 GLM-5.3 56.6(8/28 已开放权重)都高于 Flash。原作者很可能是被 Epoch 过时的 accessibility 字段误导(GLM-5.3 标 API access,MiMo-V2.6 留空)。另外 Epoch 把 Kimi K3 标为 'Open weights (non-commercial)'。各项 SE 约 4.4–5.6pp。(e) OSWorld 2:Opus 5 (max) 31.43%,用的是 batch tool 设置、500 步;开源最好是 MiniMax-M3 和 Kimi K2.6 的 4.6%,用的是 standard 设置。31.43/4.6=6.83x,算术无误,但口径不对等:工具设置不同(batch vs standard),开源样本只有 4–6 月的老模型(K2.6、M3、Qwen3.7-plus),Kimi K3、GLM-5.x、DeepSeek V4 都没测。同为 standard 设置时,Opus 4.8 max 18.5 对 4.6 是 4.0x;同期的 Opus 4.7 standard 13.9(4/16)对 Kimi K2.6(4/20)4.6 是 3.0x。老 OSWorld 的 Sonnet 4.6 72.1 和 Kimi K2.5 63.3 在 Epoch os_world_external 里吻合。(f) andonlabs 页面原文:'Chinese: +$1,047/month (R² = 0.98) · Western: +$822/month (R² = 0.95) · Chinese lags by ~111 days · Projected crossover: Oct 2027',逐字吻合。Epoch 转录 GPT-6 Astra 15514.70,GLM-5.2 8313.78,四舍五入后吻合。注意:这里的 Chinese 指中国 SOTA 线,包括闭源模型,不等于开源;111 天是两条拟合线之间的时移。按原始 SOTA 算,中国最高分 8,314 自 6/16 以来没有被超过(GLM-5.3 是 8,164),而西方从 4 月 Opus 4.7 的 10,937 涨到 15,515。也就是说截至 2026-10,中国 SOTA 只相当于西方 2026 年 4 月前后的水平,原始滞后约 6 个月,比 111 天大。2027-10 交叉是线性外推;Opus 5.5 9,235 低于 Opus 5 11,182,说明单点噪声很大。

# G20 — 票型 ['CORRECTED', 'CORRECTED', 'CORRECTED']

## 票 1:CORRECTED(单源已核)

**修正后表述**:τ²-bench banking_knowledge(Sierra 2026-08-04 批次,97 个任务、4 trials、alltools 检索、gpt-5.2 低推理用户模拟器、seed 300)上,Opus 5 pass^1/pass^4 为 48.7/32.0,GLM-5.2 为 37.1/13.4,Kimi K3 为 37.1/17.5:pass^1 差 1.31x,pass^4 差拉大到 1.8–2.4x,说明一致性差距大于单次成功率差距。但榜首是 Qwen 3.8 Max(55.2/35.1),它基于随后开放权重的 Qwen3.8-2.4T-A95B,所以'开放模型可靠性全面落后'不成立;美国的 Muse Spark、Inkling 保持率(0.45–0.51)也和 GLM/Kimi 相近。老域里开放模型已追平甚至反超:telecom 上 Qwen3.5-397B 为 97.8/92.1,Opus 4.5 为 92.3/78.1;airline 上 GLM-5 为 82.5/70.0,Opus 4.5 为 84.0/70.0。τ-bench 原论文早就指出 gpt-4o 级智能体'pass^8 <25% in retail'。注意:pass^k 同时反映被测模型和用户模拟器的随机性,小样本下 pass^4 的差距误差大。

**口径修正**:
- 判死:'banking 第一为阿里闭源 Qwen 3.8 Max' → Qwen 3.8 Max 基于 2026-08-08 开放权重的 Qwen3.8-2.4T-A95B(定制许可);因此 banking 榜首(55.2/35.1)是中国开放权重系模型,pass^1 与 pass^4 都高于 Opus 5——这与'开源可靠性落后'的叙事直接冲突,须写入
- (a) 1.31x / 1.83–2.39x 复算成立,但须注明 97 个任务、4 trials、单一 seed,pass^4 只对应约 13–31 个任务,误差大;Opus 5 不是闭源第一(同批 Grok 4.5 为 47.9/32.0)
- (b) GLM-5 的 0.32 来自不同检索配置(text-emb-3-large)、pass^1 只有 9.8%,应删去或单列;Opus 4.5 为 high 档、Opus 5 为 max 档;美国的 Muse Spark 1.1(0.51)与 Inkling(0.45)保持率同样低,不能写成开源/闭源分界
- (c) 成立,须注明是 2026-02/03 批次、Opus 4.5 high 档
- (e) 成立,限定为 τ-bench v1、gpt-4o 时代(2024)
- 补充:用户模拟器(gpt-5.2 low、seed 300)的随机性计入 pass^k,会混入跨模型比较

**核对过程**:复算方法:读取每个 submission.json 的 results.*.pass_1/pass_4,算 pass4/pass1。(a) banking_knowledge(alltools 检索,user_simulator 'gpt-5.2';notes 原文'GPT-5.2 low-reasoning user simulation, four trials, and seed 300'):Opus 5 max 48.71/31.96;GLM-5.2 xhigh 37.11/13.40;Kimi K3 max 37.11/17.53。pass^1 比 1.313,pass^4 比 2.385(对 GLM)和 1.826(对 Kimi),均对。共 97 个任务,pass^4 13.4% 只相当于约 13 个任务,样本小,置信区间宽。(b) 保持率复算:Opus 4.5 为 11.34/24.74=0.458(注意是 high 档,Opus 5 是 max 档);Opus 5 为 0.656;GPT-5.2 为 0.576;GPT-5.5 为 0.671;GPT-5.6 Sol 为 0.593;GLM-5.2 为 0.361;Kimi K3 为 0.472,均对。GLM-5 为 3.09/9.79=0.316,但该条 retrieval_config='text-emb-3-large',不是 alltools,pass^1 只有 9.8%,和其他条目不可比。反例:美国模型 Muse Spark 1.1 只有 0.51,Inkling 0.45;Qwen 3.8 Max 为 0.64,和闭源相当。所以'开源保持率低'并不是干净的开源/闭源分界。(c) telecom:Qwen3.5-397B 97.81/92.11 vs Opus 4.5 92.32/78.07;airline:GLM-5 82.5/70.0 vs Opus 4.5 84.0/70.0,均对(2026-02/03 批次,user sim 同为 gpt-5.2)。(d) banking 第一名是 Qwen 3.8 Max xhigh 55.15/35.05,数字对;但'阿里闭源'站不住:HF 上 Qwen3.8-2.4T-A95B(2026-08-08,qwen3.8-max 许可)的 README 写'Qwen3.8-Max is the official version based on Qwen3.8-2.4T-A95B',即开放权重,API 版另加视觉、1M 上下文等功能;τ² 提交日期是 2026-08-04,早于权重发布 4 天。(e) τ-bench 摘要逐字:'succeed on <50% of the tasks, and are quite inconsistent (pass^8 <25% in retail)',主语是 gpt-4o 等 2024 年模型,对。混杂因素:所有 banking 条目共用一个 gpt-5.2 低推理用户模拟器和单一 seed 300,pass^k 同时包含被测模型和用户模拟器两层随机性;用户模拟器出错会同等压低所有模型的 pass^k,并压缩不同模型之间的保持率差距。RAFT 条目用的是 claude-sonnet 模拟器,不可比。

来源:github.com/sierra-research/tau2-bench web/leaderboard/public/submissions/*/submission.json(sparse clone 后用 python 全量抽取);https://export.arxiv.org/abs/2406.12045;HF Qwen/Qwen3.8-2.4T-A95B README;epoch all_ai_models.csv

## 票 2:CORRECTED(单源已核)

**修正后表述**:在 τ²-bench 新的 banking_knowledge 域(Sierra 2026-08-04 统一批次:4 trials,gpt-5.2 用户模拟器,seed 300,约 97 题)上,Claude Opus 5 的 pass^1/pass^4 为 48.7/32.0,开源 GLM-5.2 为 37.1/13.4、Kimi K3 为 37.1/17.5。单次成功率只差 1.31 倍,四次全对的比率差到约 1.8–2.4 倍;后者只基于 13–31 题的计数,区间较宽。闭源模型的 pass^4/pass^1 保持率多在 0.57–0.73(GPT-5.5 0.67、Opus 5 0.66、Grok 4.5 0.67、中国闭源 Qwen 3.8 Max 0.64),开源多在 0.32–0.47。这更像开源/闭源与能力水平的分化,不是中美分化,且 Anthropic 内部也不单调(Opus 4.6 仅 0.42)。在接近饱和的老域上,开源已追平甚至超过:telecom 上 Qwen3.5-397B 97.8/92.1 对 Opus 4.5 92.3/78.1,airline 上 GLM-5 82.5/70.0 对 Opus 4.5 84.0/70.0。banking 第一是阿里闭源 Qwen 3.8 Max(55.2/35.1)。τ-bench 原论文早就指出 gpt-4o 一代在 retail 上 pass^8 不足 25%。pass^k 同时吸收 LLM 用户模拟器的随机性,跨批次比较必须统一模拟器。

**口径修正**:
- (a) 数字全对;须注明 banking 约 97 题,pass^4 只有 13–31 题的计数,2.39x 的置信区间很宽(粗估约 1.3–4.5x),应写成“约 1.8–2.4 倍(小样本)”
- (b) 保持率数字全对;须注明 Opus 4.5 为 high 档、Opus 5 为 max 档,Anthropic 系列不单调(Opus 4.6 0.42、Opus 4.8 0.57),保持率与 pass^1 水平相关;中国闭源 Qwen 3.8 Max 0.64 与美国闭源相当,应写开源/闭源分化,而非中美分化
- (c) 吻合;须注明这是老域,接近饱和(telecom pass^1 > 90),说明老域上开源可靠性已追平
- (d) 吻合;Qwen 3.8 Max 是阿里闭源,不是开源
- (e) 吻合,对象为 gpt-4o 一代
- 补充:跨批次比较须统一 user simulator(2026 年 Sierra 批次用 gpt-5.2 低推理档;老条目用 gpt-4.1 或自家模型模拟),否则 pass^k 不可比

**核对过程**:复算全部 text submissions:(a) banking_knowledge 2026-08-04 Sierra 批次(tau2 v1.0.1,user simulator gpt-5.2 低推理档,4 trials,seed 300,AllTools 检索):Claude Opus 5 (max) 48.71/31.96;GLM-5.2 (xhigh) 37.11/13.40;Kimi K3 (max) 37.11/17.53。48.71/37.11=1.31x;31.96/17.53=1.82x,31.96/13.40=2.39x,吻合。由 48.711=189/388 反推,该域约 97 题,所以 pass^4 实际是 31/97、13/97、17/97 题的计数。按独立泊松近似,log 比值 SE 约 sqrt(1/31+1/13)≈0.33,2.39x 的 95% 区间约为 1.3–4.5x,很宽;配对 trajectory 未随仓库发布,做不了配对 bootstrap。(b) 保持率 pass^4/pass^1:Opus 4.5 0.46(high 档,2026-05-05 按 v1.0.1 重评)、Opus 5 0.66(max 档);GPT-5.2 0.58(high 档;none 档为 0.33)、GPT-5.5 0.67、GPT-5.6-sol 0.59;GLM-5 0.32(pass^1 仅 9.8)、GLM-5.2 0.36、Kimi K3 0.47,全部吻合。反证与混杂:Anthropic 内部并不单调(Opus 4.6 0.42 → 4.7 0.62 → 4.8 0.57 → Opus 5 0.66;Fable 5 0.73);Opus 4.5 与 Opus 5 推理档位不同;保持率随 pass^1 水平机械变化(低 pass^1 时 pass^4 更易归零,如 Gemini 2.5 Pro 0.08);中国闭源 Qwen 3.8 Max 0.64、xAI Grok 4.5 0.67 与美国闭源相当,Meta Muse Spark 1.1 0.51、Thinking Machines Inkling 0.45 与开源相当。所以分化更像开源/闭源加能力水平,而非国别。(c) telecom:Qwen3.5-397B-A17B 97.8/92.1,Opus 4.5 92.32/78.07;airline:GLM-5 82.5/70.0,Opus 4.5 84.0/70.0,吻合,且同为 gpt-5.2 user simulator。另:Qwen3-Max-Thinking telecom 98.2/93.9 用的是 gpt-4.1 simulator,不可混比。(d) banking 第一 Qwen 3.8 Max 55.15/35.05,吻合。(e) arXiv 摘要原文含 "(pass^8 <25% in retail)",对应 gpt-4o 等函数调用 agent,吻合。user simulator 混杂:pass^k 把 agent 失败和 LLM 用户模拟器的随机偏离一起计入;banking 全批次统一用 gpt-5.2 低档、seed 300,但老 τ-bench 条目混用 gpt-4.1、DeepSeek-V3.2、Gemini-3.0-Pro、claude-sonnet-4.5 作模拟器,跨批次 pass^k 不可比。

来源:github.com/sierra-research/tau2-bench main 分支 tarball → web/leaderboard/public/submissions/*/submission.json(用 python 全量抽取 pass_1..pass_4、user_simulator、reasoning_effort);https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.12045

## 票 3:CORRECTED(单源已核)

**修正后表述**:在 τ²-bench 的 banking_knowledge 域(Sierra 2026-08 批次,97 题×4 trials,统一用 GPT-5.2 low 用户模拟器):Claude Opus 5 (max) pass^1 48.7、pass^4 32.0;GLM-5.2 37.1/13.4,Kimi K3 37.1/17.5。pass^1 只差 1.31 倍,pass^4 差 1.8–2.4 倍(小样本,SE 约 4pp)。在 pass^1 相近(37–41)的模型中,开源的 pass^4/pass^1 保持率(0.36–0.47)低于多数闭源(0.51–0.73),但闭源内部也有低保持率的(Gemini 3 Pro 0.23);保持率受 pass^1 水平和推理档位影响,不宜做代际直比。老域上开源并不差:telecom 上 Qwen3.5-397B 97.8/92.1 高于 Opus 4.5 的 92.3/78.1。banking 第一是阿里闭源的 Qwen 3.8 Max(55.2/35.1)。τ-bench 原论文已指出 gpt-4o 级 agent 在 retail 上 pass^8 <25%。pass^k 含用户模拟器的随机性,只能在同模拟器批次内比较。

**口径修正**:
- (a) 数字全对;要补『97 题、4 trials,pass^4 GLM-5.2 13/97 对 Kimi K3 17/97,SE 约 4pp』,并注明 effort:Opus 5 max、GLM-5.2 xhigh、Kimi K3 max
- (b) 保持率数字全对,但要改写:不能拿 Opus 4.5(high)→Opus 5(max)当纯代际进步(档位也变了);GLM-5 0.32 来自 pass^1 9.8 的低分区,不可比;建议只在 pass^1 37–41 区间比:开源 0.36/0.47 对闭源 0.51–0.73;并承认闭源也有低保持率(Gemini 3 Pro 0.23、Gemini 2.5 Pro 0.08)
- (c) 吻合;注明来自同一 gpt-5.2 模拟器批次,老域(telecom/airline)已近饱和
- (d) 吻合(Qwen 3.8 Max 只有 API,算闭源)
- (e) 引语逐字吻合;限定为 2024 年 gpt-4o 级 agent、retail 域
- 要加的方法学提示:pass^k 包含 user simulator 的随机性;同批次统一用 GPT-5.2 low、seed 300,可以横比,跨批次(gpt-4.1/gpt-5.5/v1.0 模拟器)不可比

**核对过程**:我下载了全部 submission.json 复算。(a) banking_knowledge 这批 submission_date 是 2026-08-04,methodology 原文写 'Banking_knowledge evaluation with AllTools retrieval, GPT-5.2 low-reasoning user simulation, four trials, and seed 300.' 读数:Opus 5 pass_1 48.71、pass_4 31.96(effort max);GLM-5.2 37.11/13.40(xhigh);Kimi K3 37.11/17.53(max)。48.71/37.11=1.31,31.96/13.40=2.39,31.96/17.53=1.82,复现无误。由 48.711×4×97/100=189 反推任务数是 97,所以 pass^4 的 13.4 对 17.5 只是 13 题对 17 题,二项 SE 约 3.5–4.7pp,比值的不确定性很大。(b) 保持率 pass^4/pass^1:Opus 4.5 0.46(effort high)、Opus 5 0.66(max)、GPT-5.2 0.58(high)、GPT-5.5 0.67、GPT-5.6 Sol 0.59(都是 xhigh)、GLM-5 0.32(9.8/3.1,effort 'enabled')、GLM-5.2 0.36、Kimi K3 0.47,全部吻合。混杂因素:① 推理档位不一致(Opus 4.5 是 high,Opus 5 是 max);② 保持率在机制上随 pass^1 变化,pass^1 越低比值越不稳也越低。GLM-5 的 pass^1 只有 9.8,0.32 主要是低分区噪声;同为 pass^1 9.8 的 Qwen3.5-397B 是 0.53。比较公平的做法是在 pass^1 相近(37–41)的区间比:开源 GLM-5.2 0.36、Kimi K3 0.47,闭源 Opus 4.8 0.57、GPT-5.4 0.55、Opus 4.7 0.62、Fable 5 0.73、Muse Spark 1.1 0.51(闭源)。方向成立,但开源与最差闭源的差距只有约 1–2 个 SE。③ 被省略的反例:Grok 4.5 是 0.67,Gemini 2.5 Pro 是 0.08、Gemini 3 Pro 是 0.23(闭源低保持率),说明不是『闭源普遍高保持率』。(c) telecom:Qwen3.5-397B 97.8/92.1 对 Opus 4.5 92.3/78.1;airline:GLM-5 82.5/70.0 对 Opus 4.5 84.0/70.0,全部吻合(同一 2026-02/03 批次,user sim 都是 gpt-5.2)。同批 GPT-5.2 telecom 89.7/71.9、Gemini 3 Pro 91.0/74.6,也都低于 Qwen3.5。注意 Qwen3-Max 2026-01 的 98.2/93.9 用的是 gpt-4.1 user sim,不能并入比较。(d) Qwen 3.8 Max 55.2/35.1(xhigh)确为 banking 第一;Epoch 把 Qwen3.8 Max 标为 API access,所以『阿里闭源』成立。(e) arXiv 2406.12045 摘要原文:'are quite inconsistent (pass^8 <25% in retail)',指的是 gpt-4o 等 2024 年的 function-calling agent。user simulator 的混杂:banking 这批统一用 GPT-5.2 low-reasoning 模拟器、seed 300,跨 agent 可比;但 pass^k 测到的是 agent 与模拟器联合的随机性,模拟器偏离剧本时会同时压低所有 agent 的 pass^k。不同批次的模拟器也不同:老提交是 gpt-4.1,语音提交是 gpt-5.5 或 v1.0,不能跨批次比较。

来源:github.com/sierra-research/tau2-bench web/leaderboard/public/submissions/*/submission.json(下载全部 70 个目录,python 复算);https://export.arxiv.org/api/query?id_list=2406.12045(摘要原文)

# G21 — 票型 ['CORRECTED', 'CORRECTED', 'CORRECTED']

## 票 1:CORRECTED(多源证实)

**修正后表述**:NIST CAISI(2026-05-01)认为开放权重的 DeepSeek V4 Pro 'lag[s] behind the frontier by about 8 months':DeepSeek 自报数据显示它接近 Opus 4.6/GPT-5.4,但 CAISI 含非公开基准的评测显示它'performs similarly to GPT-5'。分项表(GPT-5.5 / Opus 4.6 / V4 Pro)为 PortBench 78/60/44、CTF-Archive-Diamond 71/46/32(V4 一项为 IRT 推算)、ARC-AGI-2 semi-private 79/63/46(任务均分)、GPQA 96/91/90、OTIS-AIME 100/92/97,差距集中在私有和智能体类基准,知识题与数学题几乎追平。2025-09 的 V3.1 报告全文称软件工程和网络安全领域'the best U.S. model solves 20-80% more tasks',新闻稿由商务部长定调为'American AI dominates'。CAISI 评估 GLM-5.2 整体≈GPT-5.2、cyber≈Opus 4.6;GLM-5.3 cyber 约落后 4 个月(美国前沿包括 trusted-access 模型,且关闭了防护),但单项差距悬殊:SEC-Bench Pro 40.4% vs 90.2%、ExploitGym 9.4% vs 44.4%、OSS-Fuzz 7.7% vs 23.2%。UK AISI(2026-07-17)测得开放权重模型在 cyber 上落后 4–7 个月,比 2025 年的 6–10 个月更窄;长程 cyber range 上差距更大,但 AISI 自认那是较弱证据。UK AISI/CAISI 对 Kimi K3 的联合评测:32 步攻击链平均到第 17 步,最强美国模型平均 28.5 步,而美国闭源模型是在关闭系统级防护的条件下测的。

**口径修正**:
- (a) 分项是 NIST 表格中的数字,不是从图读出;须注明 V4 的 CTF 32% 为 IRT 推算、ARC-AGI-2 为任务均分(不是官方口径);'8 个月'基于 16 个基准、35 个模型的 IRT 拟合
- (b) '20-80%' 出自全文报告 PDF,只限软件工程与网络安全领域;新闻稿版本是'over 20%',引用时须标明出处
- (c) GLM-5.3 '约落后 4 个月'中的美国前沿包含 trusted-access(非公开发布)模型,且美国模型关闭了网络防护;GLM-5.3 先上 API,两周后才开放权重
- (d) AISI 自己说长程 cyber range 的对比是'weaker evidence',并提醒结论不能外推到 Mythos/GPT-5.5 的跃升;Kimi K3 的 17 vs 28.5 步是'最强美国模型平均',且美国闭源模型关闭了系统级防护——这一条属实,须写入

**核对过程**:(a) V4 Pro 页逐字:'CAISI evaluations indicate that DeepSeek V4's capabilities lag behind the frontier by about 8 months';引语'According to DeepSeek's data, DeepSeek V4 is about as capable as Opus 4.6 and GPT-5.4, which were released about 2 months ago. However, CAISI's evaluations, which include non-public benchmarks, indicate that DeepSeek V4 performs similarly to GPT-5'逐字一致,原文后面还有', which was released about 8 months ago (Figure 3)'。分项不必从图读:Figure 2 是 HTML 表格,GPT-5.5 xhigh / Opus 4.6 max / V4 Pro max 依次为 PortBench 78/60/44、CTF-Archive-Diamond 71/46/32***、ARC-AGI-2 semi-private** 79/63/46、GPQA-Diamond 96/91/90、OTIS-AIME-2025 100/92/97,全部逐字对上。脚注:'***Imputed from a subset of samples via IRT',即 V4 的 CTF 分数是推算的;'**CAISI reports mean score across tasks',与 ARC 官方口径不同。另外 Figure 1 用的是'16 benchmarks across 35 models',表格只有 9 个基准。(b) 全文 PDF 第 1 页逐字:'the best U.S. model solves 20-80% more tasks than the best DeepSeek model',只限 software engineering 和 cyber 两个领域;新闻稿版本写的是'solves over 20% more tasks'。政治定调:Lutnick 原话'The report is clear that American AI dominates, with DeepSeek trailing far behind',新闻稿开头还有'Thanks to President Trump's AI Action Plan'。(c) GLM-5.2 页:'GLM-5.2's overall capabilities are similar to that of GPT-5.2 ... cyber capabilities are similar to that of Opus 4.6',对。GLM-5.3 页:'lags the capability level of the U.S. frontier by about four months';表格 SEC-Bench Pro 40.4% (74/183) vs 90.2%,ExploitGym 9.4% (47/498) vs 44.4% (223/502),OSS-Fuzz 7.7% vs 23.2%,均对。关键口径:'U.S. frontier best ... including both trusted-access releases and full public releases',以及'U.S. models were tested with cyber safeguards disabled'。同表 ExploitBench 为 61.1% vs 100%,PRC 此前最佳 SEC-Bench Pro 只有 27.3%,说明进步很快。(d) AISI 7-17:'trails the frontier by 4 to 7 months. This is narrower than the 6 to 10 month gap we measured in internal evaluations of open weight models released from January to September 2025',对。长程 cyber range 原文'The gap here is larger than on our narrow cyber tasks, though we treat the comparison from our ranges as weaker evidence';另有'not predictive of whether future open weight models will replicate the more recent jumps delivered by Mythos Preview and GPT-5.5'。NIST 7-23 联合页:'Kimi K3 reached step 17 of this 32-step attack path, while the most cyber-capable U.S. models reached 28.5 steps on average',以及'U.S. closed-weight models were evaluated with system-level safeguards disabled ... Publicly available versions of these models have these safeguards enabled',所以'关闭了系统级防护'属实。另外 Kimi K3 的总体 cyber 指数只来自 ExploitBench 单一基准(CI 较宽),发布时间为 Jul 23(Aug 28 更新)。

来源:https://www.nist.gov/news-events/news/2026/05/caisi-evaluation-deepseek-v4-pro;https://www.nist.gov/news-events/news/2025/09/caisi-evaluation-deepseek-ai-models-finds-shortcomings-and-risks + https://www.nist.gov/document/caisi-evaluation-deepseek-ai-models-report (PDF 69 页,pdftotext);https://www.nist.gov/news-events/news/2026/07/caisi-assessment-zais-glm-52;https://www.nist.gov/news-events/news/2026/09/caisis-assessment-zais-glm-53-cyber-capabilities;https://www.nist.gov/news-events/news/2026/07/uk-aisi-caisi-preliminary-assessment-kimi-k3s-cyber-capabilities;https://www.aisi.gov.uk/blog/how-far-behind-the-frontier-are-leading-open-weight-models-on-cyber (Jul 17, 2026)

## 票 2:CORRECTED(多源证实)

**修正后表述**:美国 CAISI 用含非公开基准的 IRT 聚合测量,2026-05-01 判定 DeepSeek V4 Pro“落后前沿约 8 个月”:按 DeepSeek 自报数据它约等于两个月前的 Opus 4.6/GPT-5.4,按 CAISI 测评只约等于 8 个月前的 GPT-5。分项(GPT-5.5 / Opus 4.6 / V4 Pro)为 PortBench 78/60/44、CTF-Archive-Diamond 71/46/32(V4 为 IRT 插补)、ARC-AGI-2 半私有集均值 79/63/46、GPQA 96/91/90,但 OTIS-AIME 上 V4 以 97 反超 Opus 4.6 的 92。2025-09 的 V3.1 报告称最佳美国模型在软件工程和网络安全上多解出 20–80% 的任务(相对值),商务部长的新闻稿措辞明显带政治定调。2026-07 CAISI 评 GLM-5.2:整体约等于 GPT-5.2,网络安全约等于 Opus 4.6。2026-09 评 GLM-5.3:网络安全聚合落后约 4 个月,但单项差距悬殊(SEC-Bench Pro 40.4% vs 90.2%、ExploitGym 9.4% vs 44.4%、OSS-Fuzz 7.7% vs 23.2%)。这里的“美国最佳”是逐项取 CAISI 测过的美国模型最高分(含 trusted-access 版本、关闭防护后测得)。英国 AISI 2026-07-17 称开源在网络安全上落后闭源前沿 4–7 个月(2025 年大部分时间为 6–10 个月),长程 cyber range 上差距更大,并提醒这不能预测后续。UK AISI 与 CAISI 2026-07-23 的联合初评显示,在 32 步攻击路径上 Kimi K3 平均到第 17 步,最强美国模型(关闭系统级防护)平均 28.5 步。

**口径修正**:
- (a) 引语与分项全部吻合;分项来自 NIST 页面表格,不是读图。须注明 V4 的 CTF 32% 是 IRT 插补值,ARC-AGI-2 用均值聚合、非官方口径,OTIS-AIME 上 V4 97 反超 Opus 4.6 92
- (b) "20-80% more tasks" 出自完整报告 PDF,新闻稿写的是 "over 20% more";这是相对增幅,不是百分点。政治定调(Lutnick「adversary AI」「American AI dominates」)吻合
- (c) GLM-5.2 与 GLM-5.3 数字吻合;须注明「美国前沿」是逐基准取 CAISI 测过的任一美国模型最高分,含 trusted-access 版本,且关闭网络安全防护后测得;GLM-5.3 权重是发布两周后才开放
- (d) AISI 4–7 个月(2025 年为 6–10 个月,即 "through most of 2025" 的内部评估)、长程 range 差距更大,均吻合;Kimi K3 的 17 vs 28.5 步出自 UK AISI 与 CAISI 联合初步评估(2026-07-23,NIST 发布),不是 AISI 单独;测美国模型时关闭系统级防护,属实
- 对立量尺:Stanford 2026 AI Index 公开榜差距 2.7%(二手转引,未回一手),应并列说明量尺差异

**核对过程**:(a) NIST 2026-05-01 原文:"CAISI evaluations indicate that DeepSeek V4's capabilities lag behind the frontier by about 8 months";"According to DeepSeek's data, DeepSeek V4 is about as capable as Opus 4.6 and GPT-5.4, which were released about 2 months ago. However, CAISI's evaluations, which include non-public benchmarks, indicate that DeepSeek V4 performs similarly to GPT-5, which was released about 8 months ago"。分项不必从图读,页面有 HTML 表(列为 GPT-5.5 xhigh / GPT-5.4 mini / Opus 4.6 max / V4 Pro max):CTF-Archive-Diamond 71/32/46/32***、PortBench 78/41/60/44、GPQA-Diamond 96/87/91/90、ARC-AGI-2 semi-private** 79/–/63/46、OTIS-AIME-2025 100/90/92/97,全部吻合。脚注:V4 的 CTF 32% 是 "Imputed from a subset of samples via IRT";ARC-AGI-2 报 "mean score across tasks",与官方聚合方式不同。反向细节:OTIS-AIME 上 V4 97 高于 Opus 4.6 92。IRT 聚合图用 16 个基准、35 个模型,表格只有 9 个基准。(b) 2025-09-30 新闻稿正文写 "solves over 20% more tasks";"20-80% more" 出自完整报告 PDF 第 20 行(pdftotext 核到)。这是相对任务数增幅,不是百分点。政治定调:Lutnick 原话含 "groundbreaking evaluation of American vs. adversary AI" 和 "American AI dominates, with DeepSeek trailing far behind",并称评测响应 Trump 的 AI Action Plan,吻合。(c) GLM-5.2(2026-07-17):"GLM-5.2's overall capabilities are similar to that of GPT-5.2, released in December 2025. GLM-5.2's cyber capabilities are similar to that of Opus 4.6, released in February 2026.",吻合。GLM-5.3(2026-09-17):"lags the capability level of the U.S. frontier by about four months in an aggregate measure";SEC-Bench Pro 40.4% (74/183) vs 90.2% (165/183);ExploitGym (Userspace) 9.4% (47/498) vs 44.4% (223/502);OSS-Fuzz 7.7% (23/297) vs 23.2% (69/297),吻合。口径:"U.S. frontier best" 是每个基准分别取 CAISI 测过的任一美国模型的最高分,"including both trusted-access releases",且 "U.S. models were tested with cyber safeguards disabled",所以对照方是逐项取最高的复合体。(d) UK AISI 2026-07-17 原文:GLM-5.2 与 DeepSeek V4-Pro "perform similarly to frontier closed models released 4 to 7 months before them – a narrower gap than the 6 to 10 months we measured through most of 2025";窄任务约等于 Opus 4.6(差 4 个月),长程 cyber range 约等于 Opus 4.5(差 7 个月),所以长程差距更大,吻合。AISI 自己注明 "not predictive",因为 Mythos Preview 与 GPT-5.5 的跳跃发生在其后。Kimi K3 那篇是 UK AISI 与 CAISI 联合评估,发在 NIST 页,日期 2026-07-23:"Kimi K3 reached step 17 of this 32-step attack path, while the most cyber-capable U.S. models reached 28.5 steps on average"(GLM-5.2 为第 11 步);并写明 "U.S. closed-weight models were evaluated with system-level safeguards disabled to reduce refusals and enable measurement of maximal capabilities",所以「关闭防护」属实。反证:secondary 报道引用 Stanford 2026 AI Index,称公开榜上中美差距仅 2.7%,与 CAISI 的 8 个月口径冲突,反映量尺差异(私有基准加 IRT,对比公开榜)。

来源:https://www.nist.gov/news-events/news/2026/05/caisi-evaluation-deepseek-v4-pro;https://www.nist.gov/news-events/news/2025/09/caisi-evaluation-deepseek-ai-models-finds-shortcomings-and-risks;https://www.nist.gov/document/caisi-evaluation-deepseek-ai-models-report(PDF,pdftotext);https://www.nist.gov/news-events/news/2026/07/caisi-assessment-zais-glm-52;https://www.nist.gov/news-events/news/2026/09/caisis-assessment-zais-glm-53-cyber-capabilities;https://www.aisi.gov.uk/blog/how-far-behind-the-frontier-are-leading-open-weight-models-on-cyber;https://www.nist.gov/news-events/news/2026/07/uk-aisi-caisi-preliminary-assessment-kimi-k3s-cyber-capabilities

## 票 3:CORRECTED(多源证实)

**修正后表述**:美国 CAISI 2026-05-01 评测开源的 DeepSeek V4 Pro,结论是落后前沿约 8 个月(IRT 综合 16 基准×35 模型)。DeepSeek 自报数据显示它与约 2 个月前发布的 Opus 4.6、GPT-5.4 相当;CAISI 用含非公开基准的测试,认为它只相当于约 8 个月前的 GPT-5。分项(GPT-5.5 xhigh / Opus 4.6 max / V4 Pro max):PortBench 78/60/44,CTF-Archive-Diamond 71/46/32(V4 为插补值),ARC-AGI-2 半私有集任务均分 79/63/46,GPQA 96/91/90,OTIS-AIME 100/92/97(数学上 V4 反超 Opus 4.6)。2025-09 的 V3.1 报告称软件工程与网络任务上美国最佳模型多解 20–80% 的题,发布时伴随明显的政治定调。CAISI 认为 GLM-5.2 整体≈GPT-5.2、网络能力≈Opus 4.6;GLM-5.3 在网络综合指数上约落后 4 个月,但单项对『美国前沿最佳』(逐基准取所有美国模型含受信访问版的最高分,且关闭防护)仍有大差距:SEC-Bench Pro 40.4% vs 90.2%,ExploitGym 9.4% vs 44.4%,OSS-Fuzz 7.7% vs 23.2%。英国 AISI(2026-07-17)测得 GLM-5.2 在网络能力上落后前沿 4–7 个月(2025 年为 6–10 个月),长程 cyber range 上差距偏大,但 AISI 自认这部分证据较弱。UK AISI 与 CAISI 7/23 联合初评 Kimi K3:在 32 步攻击路径上平均到第 17 步,美国最强模型平均 28.5 步;测美国闭源模型时关闭了系统级防护,公开版本是开启的。

**口径修正**:
- (a) 两段引语逐字吻合;分项来自 NIST 页面的 Figure 2 表格(不是从图上读的),全部吻合;要补注:V4 的 CTF 32% 是 IRT 插补,ARC-AGI-2 是任务平均分而非官方口径,推理档位为 GPT-5.5 xhigh / Opus 4.6 max / V4 max;8 个月出自 16 基准×35 模型的 IRT 趋势线(本次是 9 基准);OTIS-AIME 上 V4 高于 Opus 4.6
- (b) 改为:『新闻稿称软件工程与网络任务上美国最佳模型多解 20% 以上,完整报告称 20–80%』,范围仅限 SWE 和 cyber;政治定调有 Lutnick 引语为证
- (c) 吻合;要补口径:『U.S. frontier best』是逐基准取所有美国模型(含受信访问版)的最高分包络,且关闭了 cyber 防护,因此单项差距是上包络,高估了对任何单一公开模型的差距;4 个月是 IRT 综合指数,与单项 2–4 倍差距并不矛盾
- (d) 4–7 个月特指 GLM-5.2(窄任务 4 个月,range 约 7 个月);『长程 range 差距更大』要加 AISI 自己的限定(证据较弱、range 数量少);Kimi K3 那份是 UK AISI 与 CAISI 的联合初评(7/23),K3 的综合能力只由 ExploitBench 单一基准估计;『测美国模型时关闭系统级防护』属实,原文明说公开版本防护是开启的

**核对过程**:(a) NIST 2026-05-01 原文:'CAISI evaluations indicate that DeepSeek V4's capabilities lag behind the frontier by about 8 months (Figure 1)'。另一句也逐字吻合:'According to DeepSeek's data, DeepSeek V4 is about as capable as Opus 4.6 and GPT-5.4, which were released about 2 months ago. However, CAISI's evaluations, which include non-public benchmarks, indicate that DeepSeek V4 performs similarly to GPT-5',原文后面还接着 'which was released about 8 months ago'。分项数字不是从图上读的,页面上有 Figure 2 的 HTML 表格可以直接核对,列为 GPT-5.5 (xhigh) / GPT-5.4 mini / Opus 4.6 (max) / V4 Pro (max):CTF-Archive-Diamond 71/32/46/32***;PortBench 78/41/60/44;GPQA-Diamond 96/87/91/90;ARC-AGI-2 semi-private** 79/–/63/46;OTIS-AIME-2025 100/90/92/97;IRT Elo 1260/749/999/800。五项全部吻合。三条脚注口径:CTF 上 V4 的 32% 是 '***Imputed from a subset of samples via IRT';ARC-AGI-2 是 'mean score across tasks, which differs from ARC-AGI-2's official score aggregation';SWE-Bench Verified 81/79/74 被省略了。另外 Figure 1 的『8 个月』用的是 '16 benchmarks across 35 models',而本次评测是 9 个基准,口径不同。OTIS-AIME 上 V4 (97) 高于 Opus 4.6 (92),这是反向证据,应当保留。(b) 2025-09-30 新闻稿原文是 'solves over 20% more tasks';完整报告 PDF 第 20 行写 'The gap is largest for software engineering and cyber tasks, where the best U.S. model solves 20-80% more'。20-80% 出自报告而非新闻稿,而且只限于软件工程和网络安全两个领域,不是全部基准。政治定调的原文:Lutnick 'The report is clear that American AI dominates, with DeepSeek trailing far behind',以及 'Thanks to President Trump's AI Action Plan';评测本身是应 AI Action Plan 的指令做的。(c) GLM-5.2 页面日期 2026-07-17(评估在 7/8 完成),原文:'overall capabilities are similar to that of GPT-5.2, released in December 2025' 和 'cyber capabilities are similar to that of Opus 4.6, released in February 2026',吻合。GLM-5.3 页面 2026-09-17,原文 'lags the capability level of the U.S. frontier by about four months';表格:SEC-Bench Pro 40.4% (74/183) 对 90.2%,ExploitGym (Userspace) 9.4% (47/498) 对 44.4%,OSS-Fuzz 7.7% 对 23.2%,还有 ExploitBench 61.1% 对 100%,吻合。关键口径:原文定义 'U.S. frontier best' 为 'the highest score on that benchmark achieved by any model released by a United States entity, including both trusted-access releases and full public releases',即逐基准取所有美国模型(含仅限受信访问的模型)的最高分包络,并非单一模型;并且 'when applicable, U.S. models were tested with cyber safeguards disabled'。(d) AISI 7/17 原文:'it trails the frontier by 4 to 7 months. This is narrower than the 6 to 10 month gap we measured in internal evaluations of open weight models released from January to September 2025'。但这个 4–7 个月特指 GLM-5.2:窄任务上相当于 Opus 4.6(约 4 个月),cyber range 上相当于 Opus 4.5(约 7 个月)。DeepSeek V4-Pro 在窄任务上落后约 5 个月,在 TLO 上低于 Sonnet 4.5(7 个月)。原文说 range 上 'The gap here is larger',但同时称 'we treat the comparison from our ranges as weaker evidence',还说这个结论 'not predictive of whether future open weight models will replicate the more recent jumps delivered by Mythos Preview and GPT-5.5'。Kimi K3 是 7/23 的 UK AISI / CAISI 联合初评,不是 AISI 单独做的。原文:'Kimi K3 reached step 17 of this 32-step attack path, while the most cyber-capable U.S. models reached 28.5 steps on average',吻合,GLM-5.2 是 step 11;10 次尝试中 K3 完成 1 次;ExploitBench ACE 0/41 对 20/41。关于防护是否关闭,原文:'U.S. closed-weight models were evaluated with system-level safeguards disabled to reduce refusals and enable measurement of maximal capabilities. Publicly available versions of these models have these safeguards enabled.' 所以确实关闭了。另外,K3 的总体 cyber 能力只由 ExploitBench 单一基准(41 题)估计,置信区间更宽,而且受托管设置限制只跑了部分评测,权重当时还没公开。

来源:https://www.nist.gov/news-events/news/2026/05/caisi-evaluation-deepseek-v4-pro (curl全文含表格); https://www.nist.gov/news-events/news/2025/09/caisi-evaluation-deepseek-ai-models-finds-shortcomings-and-risks (curl); https://www.nist.gov/document/caisi-evaluation-deepseek-ai-models-report (PDF 69页, pdftotext); https://www.nist.gov/news-events/news/2026/07/caisi-assessment-zais-glm-52; https://www.nist.gov/news-events/news/2026/09/caisis-assessment-zais-glm-53-cyber-capabilities; https://www.nist.gov/news-events/news/2026/07/uk-aisi-caisi-preliminary-assessment-kimi-k3s-cyber-capabilities; https://www.aisi.gov.uk/blog/how-far-behind-the-frontier-are-leading-open-weight-models-on-cyber (Jul 17, 2026)

# G22 — 票型 ['CORRECTED', 'CORRECTED', 'CORRECTED']

## 票 1:CORRECTED(单源已核)

**修正后表述**:按 Epoch 2026-10 数据自算(开源=Epoch 标注为 open weights 的模型),开源最佳与发布日不晚于它的闭源最佳之比:GPQA 0.98、SWE-bench Verified 0.94(GLM-5.2 0.787 vs Opus 4.7 0.835)、DeepSWE 0.94、OSWorld(旧版)0.95、FrontierMath T1-3 0.81、APEX-Agents 0.78(若按 AA 把 MiMo-V2.6-Pro 算作开源则约 0.87)、Terminal-Bench 2.0 0.66、HLE 0.65、FrontierSWE 0.55、METR 时间跨度 0.27。HLE、METR 等项的开源最佳是半年多前的旧模型,对比当前闭源最佳则只有 0.05–0.45。Artificial Analysis 2026-04-30 称 Kimi K2.6 和 MiMo V2.5 Pro 以 54 分并列开源第一,比 GPT-5.5(xhigh)的 60 分低 6 点(比 Gemini 3.1 Pro / Opus 4.7 低 3 点),一年前约 13 点,并写道 'The gap to proprietary remains wide on the hardest reasoning and agentic coding evaluations',前 10 开源全部来自中国实验室。2026-10-12 AA 榜单:Opus 5.5(max, with fallback)58、GPT-6 Astra 53、Gemini 4 Argon(high)53、开源最高 MiMo-V2.6-Pro 46、Kimi K3 44(指数版本已更迭,不可与 4 月数字直接相比)。Ihle(2026-05-28)估算开源在私有基准上落后闭源前沿 8–10 个月、公开基准 4–6 个月,差距在 2025 年 1 月 DeepSeek R1 前后最小、此后扩大(作者提示私有基准并非都完全私有,第三方部署降级可能放大差距)。Epoch(2026-01-02)按 ECI 计,2023 年以来前沿模型均出自美国,中国模型平均落后 7 个月(4–14 个月)。

**口径修正**:
- APEX-Agents 0.78 → 约 0.87:Epoch 元数据缺 MiMo-V2.6-Pro 的 accessibility,AA 标它为开源最高(0.595 vs Claude Fable 5.1 0.686);保留 0.78 必须注明'仅计 Epoch 标注为开源的模型'
